📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期SARS-CoV-2研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在巴基斯坦,XBB.1 及其子变体在 2023 年占主导地位,JN.1 及其子变体持续到 2024 年。虽然到 2024 年中期出现了 KP.2、LB.1 和 LA.1 变体,但 JN.1 仍然占主导地位。相比之下,美国、英国和法国的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 显示,到 2024 年 9 月,XBB.1.5 转变为 JN.1 和 KP.3.3。值得注意的是,JN.1 菌株在印度一直持续到 2024 年。巴基斯坦的基因组替换率为 0.467E-04(95CI:3.29E-04-6.04E-04),与美国、英国和法国的基因组替换率相似。法国,但高于印度。巴基斯坦的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 平均位置熵较高,为 0.0324(其他国家为 0.01-0.013)。
  • VYD222 表现出对多种新兴 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体(包括 XFG 变体)的有效中和作用。结构分析显示,VYD222 在 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体中容纳 突变,这些变体逃避了 FDA 批准的其他抗体,特别是那些出现在互补位接触残基之外的第二个外壳中的抗体。
  • 与祖先病毒株 P0 相比,SARS-CoV-2HA P16 会导致更严重的疾病,临床评分明显更高、病毒负荷更高、体重减轻更多、细胞因子风暴显着和肺部损伤明显。 [10.1080/22221751.2026.2760598]。
  • 该小组应用于新兴变体,提供了对最近抗原 进化 的机制见解,强调了改变的受体结合结构域构象的潜在作用,并揭示了 JN.1 衍生谱系和 BA.3.2 谱系之间抗体逃避特征的明显差异。 [10.1016/j.celrep.2026.118355]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Nebulised SNG001 was safe and associated with a reduction in COVID-19-related hospital admission. [10.1080/25310429.2026.2765991].
  • In Pakistan, XBB.1 and its subvariants were predominant in 2023 and JN.1 and its subvariants persisted through 2024. Whilst, variants KP.2, LB.1, and LA.1 emerging by mid-2024, JN.1 remained dominant.
  • VYD222 demonstrated potent neutralization against a broad range of emerging SARS-CoV-2 variants, including the XFG variant. [10.1371/journal.ppat.1014613].
  • Compared to the ancestral virus strain P0, SARS-CoV-2HA P16 causes more severe disease with significantly higher clinical scores, higher viral burden, more weight loss, prominent cytokine storm, and significant pulmonary injury.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Brazilian Journal of Microbiology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-20  |  DOI: 10.1007/s42770-026-02147-9  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 57, 1, 330
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

对巴基斯坦 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 菌株(2023 年至 2024 年)的回顾性分析揭示了 基因组 与其他国家相比存在差异的系统发育多样性

Retrospective analysis of SARS-CoV-2 Omicron strains (2023 to 2024) from Pakistan reveals differential phylogenetic diversity across genomes compared with other countries
作者： Akbar Kanji, Ali Raza Bukhari, Javaria Ashraf, Hassan Ghayas, Amina Ajmal, Maliha Yameen, Sadaf Balouch, Furqan Kabir, Fridah Mwendwa, Muhammad Imran Nisar
摘要中文翻译背景:严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 变体在 2023 年和 2024 年继续在全球范围内出现,但来自巴基斯坦的基因组 流行病学 受到限制。我们通过研究 基因组 的系统发育多样性和熵,将巴基斯坦的 Omicron 变异与来自西方国家美国、英国和法国以及邻国印度的分离株进行比较。方法:我们研究了 2023 年 1 月至 2024 年 9 月期间收集的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组。对于巴基斯坦,我们还重点关注了阿迦汗大学医院的 RT-PCR 阳性样本。统计分析确定 P 值 < 0.05 被认为具有显着性。使用 Pangolin v4.4 pangoLEARN 分配基于 基因组 的谱系,并使用 Nextclade v3.5.0 进行分支分配。使用 MAFFT v7.505 比对序列,并使用 IQ-TREE v2.2.0 构建最大似然系统发育树。确定替代率和香农基因组熵。结果:在巴基斯坦,XBB.1 及其亚变体在 2023 年占主导地位,JN.1 及其亚变体持续到 2024 年。虽然到 2024 年中期出现了 KP.2、LB.1 和 LA.1 变体,但 JN.1 仍然占主导地位。相比之下,美国、英国和法国的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 显示,到 2024 年 9 月,XBB.1.5 已转变为 JN.1 和 KP.3.3。值得注意的是,JN.1 菌株在印度一直持续到 2024 年。巴基斯坦的基因组替换率为 0.467E-04(95CI:3.29E-04-6.04E-04),与美国、英国的基因组替换率相似和法国,但高于印度。巴基斯坦的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 平均位置熵较高,为 0.0324(其他国家为 0.01-0.013)。结论:我们描述了巴基斯坦的 Omicron 多样性,XBB 和 JN.1 谱系菌株的持续存在直至 2024 年。这些发现提供了对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组 进化 的见解。​可用的基因组序列相对较小,限制了全球菌株之间替代率的比较。需要扩展 监测 来了解流行毒株的本地 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 变体在 2023 年和 2024 年继续在全球出现,但来自巴基斯坦的基因组 流行病学 受到限制。
设计与对象
我们通过研究 基因组 的系统发育多样性和熵,将巴基斯坦的 Omicron 变异与来自西方国家美国、英国和法国以及邻国印度的分离株进行比较。
核心方法
我们研究了 2023 年 1 月至 2024 年 9 月期间收集的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组。对于巴基斯坦,我们还重点关注了阿迦汗大学医院的 RT-PCR 阳性样本。统计分析确定 P 值 < 0.05 被认为具有显着性。使用 Pangolin v4.4 pangoLEARN 分配基于 基因组 的谱系,并使用 Nextclade v3.5.0 进行分支分配。使用 MAFFT v7.505 比对序列,并使用 IQ-TREE v2.2.0 构建最大似然系统发育树。确定替代率和香农基因组熵。
主要结果
在巴基斯坦,XBB.1 及其子变体在 2023 年占主导地位,JN.1 及其子变体持续到 2024 年。虽然到 2024 年中期出现了 KP.2、LB.1 和 LA.1 变体,但 JN.1 仍然占主导地位。相比之下,美国、英国和法国的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 显示,到 2024 年 9 月,XBB.1.5 转变为 JN.1 和 KP.3.3。值得注意的是,JN.1 菌株在印度一直持续到 2024 年。巴基斯坦的基因组替换率为 0.467E-04(95CI:3.29E-04-6.04E-04),与美国、英国和法国的基因组替换率相似。法国,但高于印度。巴基斯坦的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 平均位置熵较高,为 0.0324(其他国家为 0.01-0.013)。
解释与创新
我们描述了巴基斯坦的 Omicron 多样性,XBB 和 JN.1 谱系菌株的持续存在直至 2024 年。这些发现提供了对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组 进化 的见解。
科研与公卫意义
需要扩展 监测 来了解流行毒株的本地 传播。
局限与证据强度
可用的基因组序列相对较小,限制了全球菌株之间替代率的比较。

Retrospective analysis of SARS-CoV-2 Omicron strains (2023 to 2024) from Pakistan reveals differential phylogenetic diversity across genomes compared with other countries

Authors: Akbar Kanji, Ali Raza Bukhari, Javaria Ashraf, Hassan Ghayas, Amina Ajmal, Maliha Yameen, Sadaf Balouch, Furqan Kabir, Fridah Mwendwa, Muhammad Imran Nisar
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: SARS-CoV-2 Omicron variants continued to emerge globally in 2023 and 2024 however, genomic epidemiology from Pakistan has been limited. We compared Omicron variants in Pakistan by studying phylogenetic diversity and entropy across genomes compared with isolates from Western countries USA, UK and France, as well as neighbouring India. METHODS: We studied SARS-CoV-2 genomes collected between January 2023 and September 2024. For Pakistan, we also focused on RT-PCR positive samples from Aga Khan University Hospital. Statistical analyses identified P value < 0.05 as considered significant. Genome-based lineages were assigned using Pangolin v4.4 pangoLEARN with clade assignment using Nextclade v3.5.0. Sequences were aligned using MAFFT v7.505 and a maximum-likelihood phylogenetic tree was constructed with IQ-TREE v2.2.0. Substitution rates and Shannon genomic entropy was determined. RESULTS: In Pakistan, XBB.1 and its subvariants were predominant in 2023 and JN.1 and its subvariants persisted through 2024. Whilst, variants KP.2, LB.1, and LA.1 emerging by mid-2024, JN.1 remained dominant. In contrast, SARS-CoV-2 in US, UK and France showed XBB.1.5 transition​ed to JN.1 and KP.3.3 by September 2024. Notably, JN.1 strains persisted in India until 2024. Genomic substitution rates in Pakistan at 0.467E-04 (95CI: 3.29E-04-6.04E-04) were similar compared with those from USA, UK and France, but higher than those from India. SARS-CoV-2 mean positional entropy in Pakistan at 0.0324 was higher (other countries 0.01-0.013). CONCLUSIONS: We describe Omicron diversity in Pakistan with persistence of XBB and JN.1 lineage strains into 2024. These finding provide insights into SARS-CoV-2 genomic evolution. ​The relatively small genomic sequences available limits the comparison with substitution rates between global strains. Expanded surveillance is required to understand local transmission of pandemic strains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
SARS-CoV-2 Omicron variants continued to emerge globally in 2023 and 2024 however, genomic epidemiology from Pakistan has been limited.
Design and population
We compared Omicron variants in Pakistan by studying phylogenetic diversity and entropy across genomes compared with isolates from Western countries USA, UK and France, as well as neighbouring India.
Core methods
We studied SARS-CoV-2 genomes collected between January 2023 and September 2024. For Pakistan, we also focused on RT-PCR positive samples from Aga Khan University Hospital. Statistical analyses identified P value < 0.05 as considered significant. Genome-based lineages were assigned using Pangolin v4.4 pangoLEARN with clade assignment using Nextclade v3.5.0. Sequences were aligned using MAFFT v7.505 and a maximum-likelihood phylogenetic tree was constructed with IQ-TREE v2.2.0. Substitution rates and Shannon genomic entropy was determined.
Main results
In Pakistan, XBB.1 and its subvariants were predominant in 2023 and JN.1 and its subvariants persisted through 2024. Whilst, variants KP.2, LB.1, and LA.1 emerging by mid-2024, JN.1 remained dominant. In contrast, SARS-CoV-2 in US, UK and France showed XBB.1.5 transitioned to JN.1 and KP.3.3 by September 2024. Notably, JN.1 strains persisted in India until 2024. Genomic substitution rates in Pakistan at 0.467E-04 (95CI: 3.29E-04-6.04E-04) were similar compared with those from USA, UK and France, but higher than those from India. SARS-CoV-2 mean positional entropy in Pakistan at 0.0324 was higher (other countries 0.01-0.013).
Interpretation and novelty
We describe Omicron diversity in Pakistan with persistence of XBB and JN.1 lineage strains into 2024. These finding provide insights into SARS-CoV-2 genomic evolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
Expanded surveillance is required to understand local transmission of pandemic strains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The relatively small genomic sequences available limits the comparison with substitution rates between global strains.

Journal: Cell Reports  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-19  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.celrep.2026.118355  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 45, 12, 118355
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

用于追踪 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 JN.1 亚变体的抗原 进化 的单克隆抗体组合

A monoclonal antibody panel to track antigenic evolution of SARS-CoV-2 JN.1 subvariants
作者： Hsiang Hong, Madeline Wu, Sue Chong, Jian Yu, Ian A. Mellis, Chien-Yu Huang, Qian Wang, Yaoxing Huang, Yicheng Guo, David D. Ho
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 不断进化,产生连续的变体,对抗体介导的中和作用具有增强的抵抗力。然而,许多现有的单克隆抗体 (mAb) 源自祖先或早期的 Omicron 菌株,并且已经失去了针对最新变体的活性,限制了它们解决新兴谱系中出现的 突变 抗原效应的能力。在这里,我们通过使用 KP.3.1.1 刺突免疫幼稚、无印记的 VelocImmune 小鼠来生成 mAb 组合,并分离和表征了针对进化变体的不同表位的 11 种人类中和抗体。然后,我们将这些抗体与特征化的 mAb 整合,建立一个工具箱,用于分析新兴变体中的抗原 进化。该小组应用于新兴变体,为最近的抗原 进化 提供了机制上的见解,突出了改变的受体结合结构域构象的潜在作用。我们的数据还揭示了 JN.1 衍生谱系和 BA.3.2 谱系之间抗体逃避谱的明显差异。我们的研究结果共同建立了一个更新的 mAb 组合,用于监测 JN.1 亚变体的抗原 进化。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决进化中严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的持续问题,其中许多现有单克隆抗体对近期变异株已失去活性,限制了其解决突变在新兴谱系中出现的抗原效应的能力。
设计与对象
该研究涉及通过用 KP.3.1.1 刺突免疫幼稚、无印迹的 VelocImmune 小鼠来生成 mAb 组合,并分离和表征针对进化变体的不同表位的 11 种人类中和抗体。
核心方法
这些方法包括用 KP.3.1.1 刺突免疫小鼠,分离和表征 11 种人类中和抗体,并将这些抗体与表征的 mAb 整合,以建立一个工具箱,用于分析新兴变体中的抗原 进化。
主要结果
该小组应用于新兴变体,提供了对最近抗原 进化 的机制见解,强调了改变的受体结合结构域构象的潜在作用,并揭示了 JN.1 衍生谱系和 BA.3.2 谱系之间抗体逃避特征的明显差异。
解释与创新
研究结果建立了一个更新的 mAb 组合,用于监测 JN.1 亚变体的抗原 进化,为 突变 在新兴变体中的抗原作用提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以告知 监测 和 诊断 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体,有助于开发更有效的 治疗 和 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到特定 mAb 组合的使用以及对 JN.1 亚变体的关注的限制,这可能无法推广到其他变体。由于研究范围有限,证据强度中等。

A monoclonal antibody panel to track antigenic evolution of SARS-CoV-2 JN.1 subvariants

Authors: Hsiang Hong, Madeline Wu, Sue Chong, Jian Yu, Ian A. Mellis, Chien-Yu Huang, Qian Wang, Yaoxing Huang, Yicheng Guo, David D. Ho
Original abstract (English)
SARS-CoV-2 continues to evolve, generating successive variants with enhanced resistance to antibody-mediated neutralization. However, many existing monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) were derived from ancestral or early Omicron strains and have lost activity against recent variants, limiting their ability to resolve the antigenic effects of mutations arising in emerging lineages. Here, we generated a mAb panel by immunizing naive, unimprinted VelocImmune mice with the KP.3.1.1 spike, and isolated and characterized 11 human neutralizing antibodies targeting diverse epitopes of evolved variants. We then integrated these antibodies with characterized mAbs to establish a toolbox for profiling antigenic evolution among emerging variants. Application of this panel to emerging variants provided mechanistic insight into recent antigenic evolution, highlighting the potential role of altered receptor-binding domain conformation. Our data also revealed clear differences in antibody-evasion profiles between JN.1-derived lineages and BA.3.2 lineages. Together, our findings establish an updated mAb panel for monitoring antigenic evolution of JN.1 subvariants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the ongoing evolution of SARS-CoV-2, where many existing monoclonal antibodies have lost activity against recent variants, limiting their ability to resolve the antigenic effects of mutations arising in emerging lineages.
Design and population
The study involved generating a mAb panel by immunizing naive, unimprinted VelocImmune mice with the KP.3.1.1 spike, and isolating and characterizing 11 human neutralizing antibodies targeting diverse epitopes of evolved variants.
Core methods
The methods included immunizing mice with the KP.3.1.1 spike, isolating and characterizing 11 human neutralizing antibodies, and integrating these antibodies with characterized mAbs to establish a toolbox for profiling antigenic evolution among emerging variants.
Main results
The panel was applied to emerging variants, providing mechanistic insight into recent antigenic evolution, highlighting the potential role of altered receptor-binding domain conformation, and revealing clear differences in antibody-evasion profiles between JN.1-derived lineages and BA.3.2 lineages.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings established an updated mAb panel for monitoring antigenic evolution of JN.1 subvariants, offering new insights into the antigenic effects of mutations in emerging variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and diagnosis of SARS-CoV-2 variants, aiding in the development of more effective treatments and vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a specific mAb panel and the focus on JN.1 subvariants, which may not generalize to other variants. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited scope of the study.
DOI · PubMed · 来源DOI · PubMed · 来源
Journal: PLoS Pathogens  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-20  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.ppat.1014613  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 22, 11, e1014613
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

VYD222 的结构和功能分析:针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的广泛中和抗体。

Structural and functional analysis of VYD222: A broadly neutralizing antibody against SARS-CoV-2 variants.
作者： Meng Yuan, Brandyn R. West, William B. Foreman, Colin J. Powers, Zi-Qi Feng, Ashley L. Taylor, Xin-Ye Yu, Grace Wang, Laura M. Walker, Tyler N. Starr
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由于 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白 中广泛的 突变 导致治疗性单克隆抗体变得无效的问题。该研究试图证明 VYD222 (pemivibart) 对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的中和效力和广度有所提高,尽管存在大量病毒 进化。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 VYD222 的结构和功能分析,VYD222 是一种从 ADG20 (adintrevimab) 重新设计的人类单克隆抗体,以评估其针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体(包括最近占主导地位的 XFG 变体)的中和效力和广度。
核心方法
使用 X 射线晶体结构分析 VYD222,以了解其与 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 和 Omicron BA.5 受体结合结构域的相互作用。采用深度突变扫描来评估 VYD222 针对广泛的临床相关 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的逃逸中和作用。
主要结果
VYD222 表现出对多种新兴 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体(包括 XFG 变体)的有效中和作用。结构分析显示,VYD222 在 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体中容纳 突变,这些变体逃避了 FDA 批准的其他抗体,特别是那些出现在互补位接触残基之外的第二个外壳中的抗体。
解释与创新
这些发现为 VYD222 针对广泛的临床相关 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的逃逸中和作用提供了重要见解,为紧急变体的风险评估提供了宝贵的指导。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 具有重要意义,特别是对于免疫功能低下的个体。VYD222 的结构和功能分析可以为针对不断变化的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体开发更强大的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用X射线晶体结构和深度突变扫描,可能无法完全捕捉病毒进化的动态性质。由于依赖结构和计算方法而没有直接的体内验证,证据强度中等。

Structural and functional analysis of VYD222: A broadly neutralizing antibody against SARS-CoV-2 variants.

Authors: Meng Yuan, Brandyn R. West, William B. Foreman, Colin J. Powers, Zi-Qi Feng, Ashley L. Taylor, Xin-Ye Yu, Grace Wang, Laura M. Walker, Tyler N. Starr
Original abstract (English)
Extensive mutations in SARS-CoV-2 spike protein have rendered most therapeutic monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) ineffective. However, here we describe how VYD222 (pemivibart), a human mAb re-engineered from ADG20 (adintrevimab) exhibits substantially improved neutralization potency and breadth against SARS-CoV-2 variants despite substantial virus evolution. VYD222 received FDA Emergency Use Authorization for pre-exposure prophylaxis of COVID-19 in certain immunocompromised adults and adolescents. Here we show potent neutralization of this antibody against a broad range of emerging variants, including the recently dominant XFG. X-ray crystal structures of VYD222 in complex with the receptor-binding domains of prototype SARS-CoV-2 and Omicron BA.5 demonstrate the molecular basis of how VYD222 accommodates the mutations in SARS-CoV-2 variants that escape other previous FDA-approved antibodies. Notably, many of the mutations between ADG20 and VYD222 occur in the second shell outside the paratope contact residues. Deep mutational scanning indicates that SARS-CoV-2's ability to escape VYD222 is constrained by structural compatibility and the need to maintain receptor binding. These findings provide crucial insights into the escape-resistant neutralization of VYD222 against a broad panel of clinically relevant SARS-CoV-2 variants and offer valuable guidance for risk assessment of emergent variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the issue of therapeutic monoclonal antibodies becoming ineffective due to extensive mutations in the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein. The research sought to demonstrate the improved neutralization potency and breadth of VYD222 (pemivibart) against SARS-CoV-2 variants despite substantial virus evolution.
Design and population
The study involved structural and functional analysis of VYD222, a human mAb re-engineered from ADG20 (adintrevimab), to assess its neutralization potency and breadth against SARS-CoV-2 variants, including the recently dominant XFG variant.
Core methods
VYD222 was analyzed using X-ray crystal structures to understand its interaction with the receptor-binding domains of SARS-CoV-2 and Omicron BA.5. Deep mutational scanning was employed to evaluate the escape-resistant neutralization of VYD222 against a broad panel of clinically relevant SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Main results
VYD222 demonstrated potent neutralization against a broad range of emerging SARS-CoV-2 variants, including the XFG variant. Structural analysis revealed that VYD222 accommodates mutations in SARS-CoV-2 variants that escape other FDA-approved antibodies, particularly those occurring in the second shell outside the paratope contact residues.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide crucial insights into the escape-resistant neutralization of VYD222 against a broad panel of clinically relevant SARS-CoV-2 variants, offering valuable guidance for risk assessment of emergent variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results are significant for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of SARS-CoV-2 variants, particularly for immunocompromised individuals. The structural and functional analysis of VYD222 can inform the development of more robust therapeutic strategies against evolving SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of X-ray crystal structures and deep mutational scanning, which may not fully capture the dynamic nature of viral evolution. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on structural and computational methods without direct in vivo validation.

Modelling severe COVID-19 by adaptation of SARS-CoV-2 in Syrian hamsters.

Modelling severe COVID-19 by adaptation of SARS-CoV-2 in Syrian hamsters.
作者： Kaiming Tang, Bingpeng Yan, Chon Phin Ong, Peng Luo, Ke-Xin Li, Vincent Kwok‐Man Poon, Chris Chung‐Sing Chan, Tong-Yun Wang, Cui-Ting Luo, Jian-Piao Cai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虽然大多数感染最近的 Omicron 亚变体的患者比感染之前的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 菌株的患者病情较轻,但一些患者仍然容易患上严重的疾病和/或长期并发症。
设计与对象
The pathogenesis of and optimal countermeasures for severe COVID-19 remain incompletely understood. Readily available small animal models closely mimicking severe COVID-19 are lacking.
核心方法
In this study, we established a new Syrian hamster model resembling severe COVID-19 using a hamster-adapted SARS-CoV-2 (SARS-CoV-2HA) obtained through serial passage.
主要结果
与祖先病毒株 P0 相比,SARS-CoV-2HA P16 会导致更严重的疾病,临床评分明显更高、病毒负荷更高、体重减轻更多、细胞因子风暴显着和肺部损伤明显。
解释与创新
Using this new SARS-CoV-2HA P16 model, we showed that nirmatrelvir and molnupiravir were effective in improving the clinical and virological parameters of hamsters with severe COVID-19.

在该模型中,年龄和性别是影响疾病严重程度的关键因素。

Modelling severe COVID-19 by adaptation of SARS-CoV-2 in Syrian hamsters.

Authors: Kaiming Tang, Bingpeng Yan, Chon Phin Ong, Peng Luo, Ke-Xin Li, Vincent Kwok‐Man Poon, Chris Chung‐Sing Chan, Tong-Yun Wang, Cui-Ting Luo, Jian-Piao Cai
Original abstract (English)
While most patients infected with recent Omicron subvariants develop milder disease than those infected with preceding SARS-CoV-2 strains, some patients remain susceptible to developing severe disease and/or long-term complications. The pathogenesis of and optimal countermeasures for severe COVID-19 remain incompletely understood. Readily available small animal models closely mimicking severe COVID-19 are lacking. We have previously established a Syrian hamster model for COVID-19 that mimics mild-to-moderate disease in humans. In this study, we established a new Syrian hamster model resembling severe COVID-19 using a hamster-adapted SARS-CoV-2 (SARS-CoV-2HA) obtained through serial passage. Compared to the ancestral virus strain P0, SARS-CoV-2HA P16 causes more severe disease with significantly higher clinical scores, higher viral burden, more weight loss, prominent cytokine storm, and significant pulmonary injury. Age and sex are key factors influencing disease severity in this model. Comparing SARS-CoV-2HA P16 and P0, N_S194L was identified as a key adaptive mutation that enhances the antagonistic effect of SARS-CoV-2 nucleocapsid protein and viral replication. Using this new SARS-CoV-2HA P16 model, we showed that nirmatrelvir and molnupiravir were effective in improving the clinical and virological parameters of hamsters with severe COVID-19. This new model serves as a robust platform for investigating the pathogenesis of and countermeasures for severe COVID-19.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
While most patients infected with recent Omicron subvariants develop milder disease than those infected with preceding SARS-CoV-2 strains, some patients remain susceptible to developing severe disease and/or long-term complications.
Design and population
The pathogenesis of and optimal countermeasures for severe COVID-19 remain incompletely understood. Readily available small animal models closely mimicking severe COVID-19 are lacking.
Core methods

Main results
Compared to the ancestral virus strain P0, SARS-CoV-2HA P16 causes more severe disease with significantly higher clinical scores, higher viral burden, more weight loss, prominent cytokine storm, and significant pulmonary injury.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
This new model serves as a robust platform for investigating the pathogenesis of and countermeasures for severe COVID-19.
Limitations and evidence strength
Age and sex are key factors influencing disease severity in this model.

Omicron 期间多次 疫苗接种 和感染后的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 抗体格局

SARS-CoV-2 antibody landscape following multiple vaccinations and infections during Omicron period
作者： Yangyupei Yang, Casey L. Juntila-Raymond, Amy P. Callear, Matthew Smith, Elie-Tino Godonou, Arnold S. Monto, Emily Toth Martin, Godonou, E.-T., Monto, A. S.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to understand the effects of repeated COVID-19 vaccination on antibody responses to SARS-CoV-2 variants, addressing the incompletely understood immunogenicity, breadth, and durability of these responses.
设计与对象
The study included participants from a prospective cohort in Michigan who had received at least five COVID-19 vaccine doses between 2020 and 2025.
核心方法
使用多重测定测量血清 IgG、IgM 和 ACE2 抑制百分比,并使用回归模型评估基线调整的免疫原性、交叉反应性和制剂特异性抗体耐久性。
主要结果
包括 21 名参与者,基线免疫力较高,疫苗接种 后抗体水平增加较小。抗体景观表现出针对祖先和 Omicron 变体的广泛交叉反应性,并且在耐久性方面具有特定于配方的差异。
解释与创新
重复的 疫苗接种 保持了基线调整的免疫原性和广泛的交叉反应性抗体反应,在耐久性方面具有配方特异性差异,表明尽管免疫历史日益复杂,但重复加强继续增强体液免疫。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告说,尽管抗体水平有所减弱,但在 疫苗 后长达 300 天的时间内,各种变体的抗体水平通常仍保持相对较高,并且在耐久性方面存在特定于制剂的差异,这可以为 疫苗 策略和公共卫生政策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其前瞻性 队列研究 设计和样本量的限制,可能无法推广到更广泛的人群。证据强度中等。

SARS-CoV-2 antibody landscape following multiple vaccinations and infections during Omicron period

Authors: Yangyupei Yang, Casey L. Juntila-Raymond, Amy P. Callear, Matthew Smith, Elie-Tino Godonou, Arnold S. Monto, Emily Toth Martin, Godonou, E.-T., Monto, A. S.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Repeated COVID-19 vaccination effects on the immunogenicity, breadth, and durability of antibody responses across antigenically distinct SARS-CoV-2 variants remain incompletely understood. Method: Participants receiving >= five COVID-19 vaccine doses 2020-2025 were selected from a prospective cohort in Michigan. Serum IgG, IgM, and ACE2 percent inhibition were measured using multiplex assays. Baseline-adjusted immunogenicity, cross-reactivity, and formulation-specific antibody durability were evaluated using regression models. Result: Twenty-one participants were included (median: seven vaccine doses). For every two-fold increase in pre-vaccination IgG concentration, post-vaccination fold-rise was 34% (95% CI: 16%-48%), 43% (95% CI: 15%-62%), and 55% (95% CI: 21%-75%) lower following monovalent, bivalent, and 2023-24 vaccination, while baseline-adjusted immunogenicity showed no significant trends across successive vaccines. Antibody landscapes demonstrated broad cross-reactivity against ancestral and Omicron variants, with similar strain-specific IgG changes across ancestral, vaccine-matched, and antigenically distant strains, but greater heterogeneity in ACE2 inhibition. Formulation-specific waning models showed significant IgG declines following monovalent and bivalent vaccination (beta = -1.03, 95% CI: -1.51 to -0.55; P<0.001 and -1.00 , 95% CI: -1.71 to -0.30; P=0.007, respectively) and ACE2 inhibition declines following monovalent vaccination, while other formulations showed less consistent changes. Despite waning, antibody levels generally remained relatively high across variants up to 300 days post-vaccination, with formulation-specific differences in durability. Conclusion: Repeated COVID-19 vaccination maintained baseline-adjusted immunogenicity and broad cross-reactive antibody responses, with formulation-specific differences in durability. Higher baseline immunity was associated with smaller post-vaccination increases, suggesting repeated boosting continues to reinforce humoral immunity despite increasingly complex immune histories.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the effects of repeated COVID-19 vaccination on antibody responses to SARS-CoV-2 variants, addressing the incompletely understood immunogenicity, breadth, and durability of these responses.
Design and population
The study included participants from a prospective cohort in Michigan who had received at least five COVID-19 vaccine doses between 2020 and 2025.
Core methods
Serum IgG, IgM, and ACE2 percent inhibition were measured using multiplex assays, and baseline-adjusted immunogenicity, cross-reactivity, and formulation-specific antibody durability were evaluated using regression models.
Main results
Twenty-one participants were included, with higher baseline immunity associated with smaller post-vaccination increases in antibody levels. Antibody landscapes demonstrated broad cross-reactivity against ancestral and Omicron variants, with formulation-specific differences in durability.
Interpretation and novelty
Repeated vaccination maintained baseline-adjusted immunogenicity and broad cross-reactive antibody responses, with formulation-specific differences in durability, suggesting that repeated boosting continues to reinforce humoral immunity despite increasingly complex immune histories.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution that despite waning, antibody levels generally remained relatively high across variants up to 300 days post-vaccination, with formulation-specific differences in durability, which can inform vaccine strategies and public health policies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its prospective cohort design and the sample size, which may not be generalizable to the broader population. The evidence strength is moderate.

Misperceived norms and willingness to get a COVID-19 vaccine: a whole-population study of adults in rural Uganda.

Misperceived norms and willingness to get a COVID-19 vaccine: a whole-population study of adults in rural Uganda.
作者： Jessica M. Perkins, Sarah Sowell Van Dyk, Charles Baguma, Calista B. Pointer, Sehee Jeon, Melissa Luong, Viola Kyokunda, Patrick Gumisiriza, Emily N. Satinsky, Bernard Kakuhikire
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to understand the impact of perceived social norms on vaccine uptake in a rural Ugandan population, addressing a knowledge gap in HIV-endemic settings regarding perceptions about others' willingness to get vaccinated for COVID-19.
设计与对象
2021年至2022年对乌干达8个村庄的所有成年人进行了一项横断面调查,答复率为90%。
核心方法
Participants completed a survey assessing their personal willingness to get a COVID-19 vaccine, their perceptions of others' willingness, and related beliefs. A multivariable Poisson regression model was used to estimate associations between perceptions and personal willingness.
主要结果
Among 1561 participants, 75% were willing to get a COVID-19 vaccine. Misperceptions about others' willingness to get vaccinated were common, with one in three vaccine-hesitant participants and one in ten vaccine-willing participants incorrectly believing that fewer than half of their village would get vaccinated. Those with such misperceptions were less likely to report personal willingness to get vaccinated.
解释与创新
研究发现,对其他人 疫苗 接种情况的误解会显着影响个人意愿,这表明提供有关 疫苗 有效性的准确信息并传达大多数成年人愿意接种疫苗的信息可以提高接种率。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了解决对 疫苗 吸收的误解的重要性,以促进有关获取 疫苗 的对话并减少与 疫苗 相关的耻辱感,特别是在艾滋病毒流行环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且研究结果可能无法推广到其他人群。样本来自乌干达特定的农村地区,这可能会限制外部有效性。

Misperceived norms and willingness to get a COVID-19 vaccine: a whole-population study of adults in rural Uganda.

Authors: Jessica M. Perkins, Sarah Sowell Van Dyk, Charles Baguma, Calista B. Pointer, Sehee Jeon, Melissa Luong, Viola Kyokunda, Patrick Gumisiriza, Emily N. Satinsky, Bernard Kakuhikire
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Perceptions about others' willingness to get vaccinated for COVID-19 are understudied in HIV-endemic settings. Perceived social norms influence engagement in other protective behaviors. Information about COVID-19 vaccine norms could inform future HIV vaccination efforts. METHODS: All adults across 8 villages in Uganda completed a survey in 2021-2022. Participants reported personal willingness to take a COVID-19 vaccine (definitely yes vs any hesitance) and estimated how many village adults would take a COVID-19 vaccine ("all or almost all," "more than half," "less than half," "very few or no one," "do not know"). Participants also reported COVID-19 related beliefs, whether they knew someone who had COVID-19, personal HIV status, and sociodemographic characteristics. A multivariable Poisson regression model estimated associations between perceptions about others' willingness to get a vaccine and personal willingness to get a vaccine. RESULTS: Among 1561 participants (>90% response rate), 1163 (75%) were willing to get a COVID-19 vaccine. One in three vaccine-hesitant participants and 1 in 10 vaccine-willing participants incorrectly thought most adults in their village would not get vaccinated. Compared to participants who thought more than half of villagers were willing to get vaccinated, those who mistakenly thought fewer than half (adjusted relative risk [aRR] = 0.81; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.70-0.94) or few to none (aRR = 0.65; 95% CI: 0.50-0.85) were willing were less likely to report willingness themselves. CONCLUSIONS: Overall, one-fifth of adults mistakenly thought most other villagers were unwilling to get vaccinated. Adults with this misperception were less willing to get a COVID-19 vaccine themselves. Strategies that provide information about vaccine effectiveness and communicate that most adults are willing to get vaccinated may increase uptake. Changing perceived norms about vaccine uptake may also foster conversations about accessing vaccines and reduce vaccine-related stigma, important considerations with an HIV vaccine on the horizon.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the impact of perceived social norms on vaccine uptake in a rural Ugandan population, addressing a knowledge gap in HIV-endemic settings regarding perceptions about others' willingness to get vaccinated for COVID-19.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted among all adults in 8 villages in Uganda from 2021 to 2022, with a 90% response rate.
Core methods
Participants completed a survey assessing their personal willingness to get a COVID-19 vaccine, their perceptions of others' willingness, and related beliefs. A multivariable Poisson regression model was used to estimate associations between perceptions and personal willingness.
Main results
Among 1561 participants, 75% were willing to get a COVID-19 vaccine. Misperceptions about others' willingness to get vaccinated were common, with one in three vaccine-hesitant participants and one in ten vaccine-willing participants incorrectly believing that fewer than half of their village would get vaccinated. Those with such misperceptions were less likely to report personal willingness to get vaccinated.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that misperceptions about others' vaccine uptake significantly influenced personal willingness, suggesting that providing accurate information about vaccine effectiveness and communicating that most adults are willing to get vaccinated could increase uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of addressing misperceptions about vaccine uptake to foster conversations about accessing vaccines and reduce vaccine-related stigma, particularly in HIV-endemic settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the findings may not be generalizable to other populations. The sample was from a specific rural Ugandan setting, which may limit external validity.

A decoupled ensemble framework for real-time nowcasting of COVID-19 case numbers and transmission dynamics.

A decoupled ensemble framework for real-time nowcasting of COVID-19 case numbers and transmission dynamics.
作者： Weijia Xiong, Liping Peng, Xiaotong Huang, Tim K Tsang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决根据病例数据实时估计随时间变化的有效再生数(Rt)时右审查偏差的局限性,这对于指导 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 传播 期间的公共卫生和社会措施至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究利用香港 Omicron 暴发(2022 年 2 月至 12 月)的每日 PCR 确诊病例数,开发并评估了一个三步框架,用于将病例预测与 Rt 估计脱钩。
核心方法
使用 10 个统计、机器学习和深度学习模型的集合,可以提前 14 天预测每日 PCR 确诊病例数。然后对预测病例进行反卷积以重建感染时间线,据此估计最多提前 7 天的 Rt。
主要结果
An unweighted ensemble of top-performing models substantially improved forecast accuracy, reducing the root mean square error and the mean absolute percentage error by 10 and 9% for case numbers and by 38 and 34% for Rt.
解释与创新
这种方法通过根据预测而不是截断的病例数据生成 Rt 估计值来减轻右审查伪影,从而能够准确实时监测流行病负担和 传播 动态。
科研与公卫意义
该框架通过提供对流行病负担和 传播 动态的准确实时监测来支持及时的公共卫生决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括需要额外的数据来稳定 Rt 估计值以及整体方法中可能存在的偏差。证据强度中等。

A decoupled ensemble framework for real-time nowcasting of COVID-19 case numbers and transmission dynamics.

Authors: Weijia Xiong, Liping Peng, Xiaotong Huang, Tim K Tsang
Original abstract (English)
Timely monitoring of epidemics such as SARS-CoV-2 transmission is essential for guiding public health and social measures, yet real-time estimation of the time-varying effective reproductive number ( R t ) from case data suffers from right-censoring bias, systematically underestimating recent R t values and requiring 7-14 days of additional data to stabilize. To address this limitation, we developed and evaluated a three-step framework that decouples case forecasting from R t estimation. Daily PCR-confirmed case counts are first forecast up to 14 days ahead using an ensemble of 10 statistical, machine learning and deep learning models. Forecasted cases are then deconvolved to reconstruct infection timelines, from which R t is estimated for up to 7 days ahead. This approach mitigates right-censoring artefacts by generating R t estimates from projected rather than truncated case data. We applied this framework to Hong Kong's Omicron outbreaks (February to December 2022), incorporating 15 epidemiological, meteorological, air pollution and policy-related predictors. An unweighted ensemble of top-performing models substantially improved forecast accuracy, reducing the root mean square error and the mean absolute percentage error by 10 and 9% for case numbers and by 38 and 34% for R t . By separating predictive modelling from epidemiological inference, this framework enables accurate real-time monitoring of epidemic burden and transmission dynamics to support timely public health decision-making.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitation of right-censoring bias in real-time estimation of the time-varying effective reproductive number (Rt) from case data, which is essential for guiding public health and social measures during SARS-CoV-2 transmission.
Design and population
The study developed and evaluated a three-step framework for decoupling case forecasting from Rt estimation using daily PCR-confirmed case counts from Hong Kong's Omicron outbreaks (February to December 2022).
Core methods
Daily PCR-confirmed case counts were forecast up to 14 days ahead using an ensemble of 10 statistical, machine learning, and deep learning models. Forecasted cases were then deconvolved to reconstruct infection timelines, from which Rt was estimated for up to 7 days ahead.
Main results
An unweighted ensemble of top-performing models substantially improved forecast accuracy, reducing the root mean square error and the mean absolute percentage error by 10 and 9% for case numbers and by 38 and 34% for Rt.
Interpretation and novelty
This approach mitigates right-censoring artefacts by generating Rt estimates from projected rather than truncated case data, enabling accurate real-time monitoring of epidemic burden and transmission dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The framework supports timely public health decision-making by providing accurate real-time monitoring of epidemic burden and transmission dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the need for additional data to stabilize Rt estimates and the potential for bias in the ensemble approach. The evidence strength is moderate.

通过基于结构和亲和力的筛选鉴定 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RBD-ACE2 相互作用的小分子抑制剂

Identification of small-molecule inhibitors of the SARS-CoV-2 RBD–ACE2 interaction by structure- and affinity-based screening
作者： Hisashi Murakami, Suzuka Makita, Ryota Fukai, Taiki Ichida, Kaito Koyama, Katsumi Ohta, Yasushi Takebayashi, Atsushi Yoshimori, Naohisa Ogo, Akira Asai
摘要中文翻译摘要 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2) 刺突蛋白 的受体结合域 (RBD) 通过与血管紧张素转换酶 2 (ACE2) 相互作用介导病毒进入,使其成为 抗病毒药物 干预的有吸引力的靶点。然而,广泛且相对浅的 ACE2 结合界面对小分子抑制剂的发现提出了挑战。在本研究中,我们探索了 ACE2 结合界面之外的保守区域,作为针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RBD 的小分子抑制剂的潜在药物位点。使用 SARS-CoV-1 RBD 的结构作为结构替代物进行基于结构的虚拟筛选,并使用新建立的基于亲和力的测定法在二氮丙啶修饰的无定形碳板上评估选定的化合物。对 175 个虚拟命中的评估确定了 8 种 RBD 结合化合物。其中,在基于 Calu-3 细胞的结合测定中,只有 RABI-1 抑制 RBD-ACE2 相互作用,而不影响 ACE2 表达或细胞活力。对 RABI-1 结构类似物的评估导致了 RABI-3 的鉴定,与 RABI-1 相比,RABI-3 显示出更强的抑制活性。此外,RABI-3 在 AlphaLISA 免疫测定中抑制了 ACE2 和源自多个 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的 RBD 之间的相互作用,并抑制了 Calu-3 细胞中的假病毒感染。这些发现支持以 ACE2 结合界面之外的 RBD 区域为目标的可行性,作为识别 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 进入的小分子抑制剂的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RBD-ACE2 相互作用的小分子抑制剂,因为 ACE2 结合界面是 抗病毒药物 干预的关键目标。
设计与对象
该研究采用基于结构和亲和力的筛选方法进行,重点关注 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 及其变体,重点关注 RBD 区域及其与 ACE2 的相互作用。
核心方法
使用 SARS-CoV-1 RBD 作为结构替代物进行基于结构的虚拟筛选,并使用基于亲和力的测定在二氮丙啶修饰的无定形碳板上评估选定的化合物。
主要结果
对 175 个虚拟命中的评估确定了 8 种 RBD 结合化合物,其中 RABI-1 在基于 Calu-3 细胞的结合测定中抑制 RBD-ACE2 相互作用,而不影响 ACE2 表达或细胞活力。RABI-3是RABI-1的结构类似物,在Calu-3细胞中表现出更强的抑制活性并抑制假病毒感染。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RBD ACE2 结合界面之外的保守区域是开发病毒进入小分子抑制剂的可行策略。
科研与公卫意义
These results provide a foundation for the development of antiviral therapies targeting SARS-CoV-2 entry, which could be relevant for managing COVID-19 and its variants.
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于体外测定,可能无法完全反映体内功效或长期效果。

Identification of small-molecule inhibitors of the SARS-CoV-2 RBD–ACE2 interaction by structure- and affinity-based screening

Authors: Hisashi Murakami, Suzuka Makita, Ryota Fukai, Taiki Ichida, Kaito Koyama, Katsumi Ohta, Yasushi Takebayashi, Atsushi Yoshimori, Naohisa Ogo, Akira Asai
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The receptor-binding domain (RBD) of the severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) spike protein mediates viral entry through its interaction with angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 (ACE2), making it an attractive target for antiviral intervention. However, the broad and relatively shallow ACE2-binding interface presents a challenge for small-molecule inhibitor discovery. In the present study, we explored conserved regions outside the ACE2-binding interface as potential druggable sites for small-molecule inhibitors targeting the SARS-CoV-2 RBD. Structure-based virtual screening was performed using the structure of the SARS-CoV-1 RBD as a structural surrogate, and selected compounds were evaluated using a newly established affinity-based assay on diazirine-modified amorphous carbon plates. Evaluation of 175 virtual hits identified eight RBD-binding compounds. Among them, only RABI-1 inhibited RBD–ACE2 interaction in a Calu-3 cell-based binding assay without affecting ACE2 expression or cell viability. Evaluation of structural analogs of RABI-1 led to the identification of RABI-3, which showed stronger inhibitory activity compared with RABI-1. In addition, RABI-3 inhibited the interaction between ACE2 and RBDs derived from multiple SARS-CoV-2 variants in an AlphaLISA immunoassay and suppressed pseudovirus infection in Calu-3 cells. These findings support the feasibility of targeting regions of the RBD outside the ACE2-binding interface as a strategy for identifying small-molecule inhibitors of SARS-CoV-2 entry.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify small-molecule inhibitors of the SARS-CoV-2 RBD–ACE2 interaction, as the ACE2-binding interface is a critical target for antiviral intervention.
Design and population
The study was conducted using a structure- and affinity-based screening approach, focusing on SARS-CoV-2 and its variants, with an emphasis on the RBD region and its interaction with ACE2.
Core methods
Structure-based virtual screening was performed using the SARS-CoV-1 RBD as a structural surrogate, and selected compounds were evaluated using an affinity-based assay on diazirine-modified amorphous carbon plates.
Main results
Evaluation of 175 virtual hits identified eight RBD-binding compounds, among which RABI-1 inhibited RBD–ACE2 interaction in a Calu-3 cell-based binding assay without affecting ACE2 expression or cell viability. RABI-3, a structural analog of RABI-1, showed stronger inhibitory activity and suppressed pseudovirus infection in Calu-3 cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that targeting conserved regions outside the ACE2-binding interface of the SARS-CoV-2 RBD is a feasible strategy for developing small-molecule inhibitors of viral entry.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a foundation for the development of antiviral therapies targeting SARS-CoV-2 entry, which could be relevant for managing COVID-19 and its variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on in vitro assays, which may not fully reflect in vivo efficacy or long-term effects.

Spatiotemporal analysis of COVID-19 in Japan: The twelve epidemic waves from 2020 to 2025

Spatiotemporal analysis of COVID-19 in Japan: The twelve epidemic waves from 2020 to 2025
作者： Shiho Sato, Masayoshi Matsumoto, Chihiro Wakabayashi, Izumi Oki, Yuri Matsubara, Daisuke Matsubara, Yui Nakaizumi, Yoshihisa Aiba, Yosikazu Nakamura, Hiroshi Yanagawa
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Since the first confirmed case of COVID-19 in Japan in January 2020, multiple epidemic waves have emerged under changing legal classifications and public health measures. Although nationwide surveillance data have been continuously available, integrated analyses of temporal and geographic patterns remain limited.
设计与对象
This study described the spatiotemporal evolution of COVID-19 in Japan in relation to the timing of major policy, vaccination, variant, and surveillance changes, using weekly prefecture-level case counts from 2020 to 2025.
核心方法
使用厚生劳动省的官方数据对2020年至2025年每周的都道府县病例数进行分析,将流行期分为流行初期、扩展期和平台期。生成三维对数转换可视化来说明全国范围内的时间和区域趋势。
主要结果
我们识别出十二次疫情浪潮。早期的暴发主要集中在人口密集的城市地区,且不同地区之间缺乏同步性。在扩散期,当一种高传播力变异株出现时,出现了大规模的全国性疫情浪潮,且第5次和第7次疫情浪潮在都道府县间的峰值时间最为一致。在平台期,随着疫情被归类为“第5类传染病”,报告的疫情浪潮强度较低且持续时间较长,都道府县之间的差异也较小;由于这些数据基于哨点报告,因此不能直接与早期阶段进行比较。
解释与创新
Overall, COVID-19 in Japan evolved from localized clusters to synchronized nationwide waves with reduced regional differences across prefectures, suggesting the value of integrated spatiotemporal surveillance frameworks for future pandemic preparedness.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果警告说,综合时空 监测 对于理解和管理未来的大流行浪潮至关重要,特别是随着变异和公共卫生措施的发展。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到平台期前哨报告的依赖的限制,这可能无法与早期阶段直接比较。该分析并未考虑所有潜在的混杂因素,例如不遵守公共卫生措施或检测率的差异。

Spatiotemporal analysis of COVID-19 in Japan: The twelve epidemic waves from 2020 to 2025

Authors: Shiho Sato, Masayoshi Matsumoto, Chihiro Wakabayashi, Izumi Oki, Yuri Matsubara, Daisuke Matsubara, Yui Nakaizumi, Yoshihisa Aiba, Yosikazu Nakamura, Hiroshi Yanagawa
Original abstract (English)
Since the first confirmed case of COVID-19 in Japan in January 2020, multiple epidemic waves have emerged under changing legal classifications and public health measures. Although nationwide surveillance data have been continuously available, integrated analyses of temporal and geographic patterns remain limited. This study described the spatiotemporal evolution of COVID-19 in Japan in relation to the timing of major policy, vaccination, variant, and surveillance changes. Weekly prefecture-level case counts from 2020 to 2025 were analyzed using official data from the Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare, and the epidemic period was divided into early epidemic, expansion, and plateau phases. Three-dimensional log-transformed visualizations were generated to illustrate nationwide temporal and regional trends. We identified twelve epidemic waves. Early outbreaks were localized in densely populated urban areas with low synchrony. During the expansion phase, large nationwide waves occurred in the period when a highly transmissible variant emerged, and peak timing was most closely aligned across prefectures in the 5th and 7th waves. In the plateau phase, following the transition to “Category 5 infectious disease,” reported waves were lower and more prolonged, with smaller differences among prefectures; because these values are based on sentinel reports, they are not directly comparable with earlier phases. Overall, COVID-19 in Japan evolved from localized clusters to synchronized nationwide waves with reduced regional differences across prefectures, suggesting the value of integrated spatiotemporal surveillance frameworks for future pandemic preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Since the first confirmed case of COVID-19 in Japan in January 2020, multiple epidemic waves have emerged under changing legal classifications and public health measures. Although nationwide surveillance data have been continuously available, integrated analyses of temporal and geographic patterns remain limited.
Design and population
This study described the spatiotemporal evolution of COVID-19 in Japan in relation to the timing of major policy, vaccination, variant, and surveillance changes, using weekly prefecture-level case counts from 2020 to 2025.
Core methods
Weekly prefecture-level case counts from 2020 to 2025 were analyzed using official data from the Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare, and the epidemic period was divided into early epidemic, expansion, and plateau phases. Three-dimensional log-transformed visualizations were generated to illustrate nationwide temporal and regional trends.
Main results
We identified twelve epidemic waves. Early outbreaks were localized in densely populated urban areas with low synchrony. During the expansion phase, large nationwide waves occurred in the period when a highly transmissible variant emerged, and peak timing was most closely aligned across prefectures in the 5th and 7th waves. In the plateau phase, following the transition to “Category 5 infectious disease,” reported waves were lower and more prolonged, with smaller differences among prefectures; because these values are based on sentinel reports, they are not directly comparable with earlier phases.
Interpretation and novelty
Overall, COVID-19 in Japan evolved from localized clusters to synchronized nationwide waves with reduced regional differences across prefectures, suggesting the value of integrated spatiotemporal surveillance frameworks for future pandemic preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution that integrated spatiotemporal surveillance is crucial for understanding and managing future pandemic waves, especially as variants and public health measures evolve.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on sentinel reports in the plateau phase, which may not be directly comparable with earlier phases. The analysis does not account for all potential confounding factors, such as non-compliance with public health measures or differences in testing rates.

Predictors and prevalence of symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing four years after COVID-19: a prospective observational study

Predictors and prevalence of symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing four years after COVID-19: a prospective observational study
作者： Carl Hallberg, A. Svensson-Raskh, Monika Fagevik Olsén, Annie Svensson, Anna Törnberg, Alexandra Halvarsson, Judith Bruchfeld, Michael Runold, M. Nygren-Bonnier, Anna Svensson‐Raskh
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Dysfunctional breathing is increasingly recognised among individuals with post-COVID-19 condition (PCC), yet data on long-term prevalence and predictors are scarce. This study assessed the prevalence of symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing, as indicated by elevated Nijmegen questionnaire (NQ) scores, four years after COVID-19 illness in adults with PCC and identified associated factors and baseline predictors by comparing individuals with and without elevated NQ scores.
设计与对象
In this prospective observational study, adults with PCC, both previously hospitalised and non-hospitalised for COVID-19, were recruited from a specialised post-COVID outpatient clinic. These individuals were younger, often female, and more frequently non-hospitalised during acute COVID-19.
核心方法
对基线评估(2020-2022 年)与 2024 年数字随访调查的数据一起进行了分析。检查了组间差异,并使用多变量逻辑回归模型来识别相关因素和基线预测因素。
主要结果
在 288 名参与者中(中位随访时间为 50 个月),143 名参与者 (50%) 出现呼吸功能障碍的症状。NQ 评分升高(≥ 23)与呼吸困难严重程度更高、PCC 症状负担更大、抑郁症状和体位性心动过速综合征独立相关。
解释与创新
提示呼吸功能障碍的症状与较高的呼吸困难严重程度、较大的 PCC 症状负担、抑郁症状和体位性心动过速综合征独立相关,突出了它们在长期 PCC 中的潜在临床相关性。
科研与公卫意义
The findings suggest that dysfunctional breathing symptoms may be a significant aspect of long-term PCC, which could inform future surveillance, diagnosis, and management strategies for individuals with prolonged post-COVID-19 symptoms.
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察设计和对自我报告结果的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差。该证据是中等的,因为它基于一项没有随机对照的 队列研究 研究。

Predictors and prevalence of symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing four years after COVID-19: a prospective observational study

Authors: Carl Hallberg, A. Svensson-Raskh, Monika Fagevik Olsén, Annie Svensson, Anna Törnberg, Alexandra Halvarsson, Judith Bruchfeld, Michael Runold, M. Nygren-Bonnier, Anna Svensson‐Raskh
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dysfunctional breathing is increasingly recognised among individuals with post-COVID-19 condition (PCC), yet data on long-term prevalence and predictors are scarce. This study assessed the prevalence of symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing, as indicated by elevated Nijmegen questionnaire (NQ) scores, four years after COVID-19 illness in adults with PCC and identified associated factors and baseline predictors by comparing individuals with and without elevated NQ scores. METHOD: In this prospective observational study, adults with PCC, both previously hospitalised and non-hospitalised for COVID-19, were recruited from a specialised post-COVID outpatient clinic. Baseline assessments (2020-2022) were analysed together with data from a digital follow-up survey in 2024. Symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing was defined as a NQ score ≥ 23 at follow-up. Between group differences were examined, and multivariable logistic regression models were used to identify associated factors and baseline predictors. RESULTS: Among 288 participants (median follow-up 50 months), 143 (50%) had symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing. These individuals were younger, often female, and more frequently non-hospitalised during acute COVID-19. Baseline predictors included non-hospitalisation, higher dyspnoea severity, lower FEV1, and younger age. At four years, elevated NQ scores (≥ 23) were independently associated with higher dyspnoea severity, greater PCC-symptom burden, depressive symptoms and postural orthostatic tachycardia syndrome. Individuals with symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing performed worse in the six-minute walk test, one-minute sit to stand test, and maximal inspiratory pressure test at baseline, whereas lung function did not differ. Patient-reported outcomes were consistently worse compared with those with NQ scores < 23 and improved less over time. CONCLUSION: Half of the individuals with PCC in this cohort reported symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing four years after COVID-19, as indicated by a NQ score ≥ 23. Elevated NQ scores were associated with greater PCC-symptom burden and poorer health outcomes, highlighting their potential clinical relevance in long-term PCC. Future studies should combine patient-reported measures with objective assessment of breathing pattern and ventilatory function.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dysfunctional breathing is increasingly recognised among individuals with post-COVID-19 condition (PCC), yet data on long-term prevalence and predictors are scarce. This study assessed the prevalence of symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing, as indicated by elevated Nijmegen questionnaire (NQ) scores, four years after COVID-19 illness in adults with PCC and identified associated factors and baseline predictors by comparing individuals with and without elevated NQ scores.
Design and population
In this prospective observational study, adults with PCC, both previously hospitalised and non-hospitalised for COVID-19, were recruited from a specialised post-COVID outpatient clinic. These individuals were younger, often female, and more frequently non-hospitalised during acute COVID-19.
Core methods
Baseline assessments (2020-2022) were analysed together with data from a digital follow-up survey in 2024. Between group differences were examined, and multivariable logistic regression models were used to identify associated factors and baseline predictors.
Main results
Among 288 participants (median follow-up 50 months), 143 (50%) had symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing. Elevated NQ scores (≥ 23) were independently associated with higher dyspnoea severity, greater PCC-symptom burden, depressive symptoms and postural orthostatic tachycardia syndrome.
Interpretation and novelty
Symptoms suggestive of dysfunctional breathing were independently associated with higher dyspnoea severity, greater PCC-symptom burden, depressive symptoms and postural orthostatic tachycardia syndrome, highlighting their potential clinical relevance in long-term PCC.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that dysfunctional breathing symptoms may be a significant aspect of long-term PCC, which could inform future surveillance, diagnosis, and management strategies for individuals with prolonged post-COVID-19 symptoms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and reliance on self-reported outcomes, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single cohort study with no randomised control.

Evolution of the clinical profile and cardiac outcomes of multisystem inflammatory syndrome in children (MIS-C) across three COVID-19 waves in Central Kerala: a cross-sectional study.

Evolution of the clinical profile and cardiac outcomes of multisystem inflammatory syndrome in children (MIS-C) across three COVID-19 waves in Central Kerala: a cross-sectional study.
作者： Keerthy Kurian, Alphy Christinne Thomas, Jijo Joseph John, Alice David, Aby Varghese, Dalia Robert
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to compare the clinical profile, laboratory findings, cardiac manifestations, treatment patterns and outcomes of children diagnosed with multisystem inflammatory syndrome in children (MIS-C) across the Alpha, Delta and Omicron waves of the COVID-19 pandemic.
设计与对象
在印度喀拉拉邦中部的一家三级护理教学医院进行了一项横断面观察研究。该研究纳入了 2020 年 11 月至 2022 年 12 月期间入院的 95 名符合 MIS-C 既定诊断标准的儿童。
核心方法
根据主要的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异波将患者分为三组:Alpha(波 1)、Delta(波 2)和 Omicron(波 3)。主要结果指标包括临床特征、实验室参数、超声心动图结果、治疗 模式和疾病严重程度标记。
主要结果
在所有三波中,胃肠道症状仍然是主要表现,而呼吸道症状在后面几波中有所增加。住院时间、发烧时间和 B 型利钠肽原水平在连续几波中显着下降。心包积液也明显减少,而左心功能不全的患病率则呈下降趋势。相比之下,Omicron 波期间冠状动脉扩张显着增加。
解释与创新
The clinical phenotype of MIS-C evolved across successive COVID-19 waves, with evidence of decreasing systemic disease severity but persistent changes in cardiac involvement.
科研与公卫意义
Omicron 波期间冠状动脉扩张显着增加,凸显了持续超声心动图 监测 和长期心脏随访的重要性,即使是在大流行的临床较温和阶段。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于对不同大流行浪潮的清晰比较以及既定诊断标准的使用,证据是强有力的。

Evolution of the clinical profile and cardiac outcomes of multisystem inflammatory syndrome in children (MIS-C) across three COVID-19 waves in Central Kerala: a cross-sectional study.

Authors: Keerthy Kurian, Alphy Christinne Thomas, Jijo Joseph John, Alice David, Aby Varghese, Dalia Robert
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To compare the clinical profile, laboratory findings, cardiac manifestations, treatment patterns and outcomes of children diagnosed with multisystem inflammatory syndrome in children (MIS-C) across the Alpha, Delta and Omicron waves of the COVID-19 pandemic. DESIGN: Cross-sectional observational study. SETTING: A tertiary care teaching hospital in Central Kerala, India. PARTICIPANTS: 95 children fulfilling established diagnostic criteria for MIS-C admitted between November 2020 and December 2022 were included. Patients were categorised into three groups according to the predominant SARS-CoV-2 variant wave: Alpha (wave 1), Delta (wave 2) and Omicron (wave 3). MAIN OUTCOME MEASURES: Clinical characteristics, laboratory parameters, echocardiographic findings, treatment modalities and markers of disease severity, including duration of hospitalisation, intensive care stay, oxygen requirement, inotropic support and ventilator support. RESULTS: Gastrointestinal symptoms remained the predominant presentation across all three waves, whereas respiratory symptoms increased during the later waves. Mucocutaneous manifestations varied significantly across waves (p=0.02). The duration of hospital stay (p=0.004), duration of fever (p=0.009) and pro-B-type natriuretic peptide levels (p=0.003) decreased significantly over successive waves, suggesting reduced overall disease severity. Pericardial effusion also decreased significantly (p=0.002), while the prevalence of left ventricular dysfunction showed a declining trend. In contrast, coronary artery dilatation increased significantly during the Omicron wave (p<0.0001). Oxygen requirement declined significantly in the third wave (p=0.02), whereas intensive care stay, inotropic support and ventilator support did not differ significantly between waves. CONCLUSIONS: The clinical phenotype of MIS-C evolved across successive COVID-19 waves, with evidence of decreasing systemic disease severity but persistent changes in cardiac involvement. The marked increase in coronary artery dilatation during the Omicron wave highlights the importance of continued echocardiographic surveillance and long-term cardiac follow-up, even during clinically milder phases of the pandemic.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the clinical profile, laboratory findings, cardiac manifestations, treatment patterns and outcomes of children diagnosed with multisystem inflammatory syndrome in children (MIS-C) across the Alpha, Delta and Omicron waves of the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
A cross-sectional observational study was conducted in a tertiary care teaching hospital in Central Kerala, India. The study included 95 children fulfilling established diagnostic criteria for MIS-C admitted between November 2020 and December 2022.
Core methods
Patients were categorised into three groups according to the predominant SARS-CoV-2 variant wave: Alpha (wave 1), Delta (wave 2) and Omicron (wave 3). The main outcome measures included clinical characteristics, laboratory parameters, echocardiographic findings, treatment modalities and markers of disease severity.
Main results
Gastrointestinal symptoms remained the predominant presentation across all three waves, whereas respiratory symptoms increased during the later waves. The duration of hospital stay, duration of fever and pro-B-type natriuretic peptide levels decreased significantly over successive waves. Pericardial effusion also decreased significantly, while the prevalence of left ventricular dysfunction showed a declining trend. In contrast, coronary artery dilatation increased significantly during the Omicron wave.
Interpretation and novelty
The clinical phenotype of MIS-C evolved across successive COVID-19 waves, with evidence of decreasing systemic disease severity but persistent changes in cardiac involvement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The marked increase in coronary artery dilatation during the Omicron wave highlights the importance of continued echocardiographic surveillance and long-term cardiac follow-up, even during clinically milder phases of the pandemic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the clear comparison across distinct pandemic waves and the use of established diagnostic criteria.

严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 主蛋白酶亚基交换抑制和配体诱导二聚化的结构见解。

Structural insights into subunit exchange inhibition and ligand-induced dimerization of SARS-CoV-2 main protease.
作者： Syuan-Ting Kuo, Zhenyu Xi, Yan J Sheng, Shiqing Xu, Wenshe Ray Liu, David H Russell, Xin Yan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2主蛋白酶(M pro)的结构动力学及其对拟肽抑制剂的反应,这对于了解其对病毒多蛋白加工的治疗作用至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究采用集成质谱平台来分析 M pro 二聚化的动态性质及其拟肽抑制剂的调节,重点关注 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 及其变体。
核心方法
研究人员使用代谢 15 N 标记、天然质谱 (nMS)、氢-氘交换质谱 (HDX-MS) 和分子动力学 (MD) 模拟来检查 M pro 响应抑制剂的结构变化和二聚化动力学。
主要结果
结果表明,apo-M pro 存在于具有快速亚基交换的动态单体-二聚体平衡中,并且拟肽抑制剂(包括 nirmatrelvir、GC376 和 MPI8)在动力学上捕获二聚体状态,抑制亚基交换并稳定 M pro 的二聚体形式。
解释与创新
这些发现为抑制剂稳定的二聚化奠定了机制基础,并强调 N 指区域作为四级结构转变的动态报告者,为治疗药物对 M pro 的结构调节提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
These results provide critical structural insights that can inform the development of more effective antiviral drugs targeting SARS-CoV-2 main protease, with potential implications for improving therapeutic strategies against COVID-19.
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于使用质谱和模拟的体外分析,证据基于抽象水平的报告,可能无法完全捕捉体内动态。证据强度中等。

Structural insights into subunit exchange inhibition and ligand-induced dimerization of SARS-CoV-2 main protease.

Authors: Syuan-Ting Kuo, Zhenyu Xi, Yan J Sheng, Shiqing Xu, Wenshe Ray Liu, David H Russell, Xin Yan
Original abstract (English)
The SARS-CoV-2 main protease (M pro ) is an essential homodimeric cysteine protease that mediates viral polyprotein processing and represents a primary therapeutic target for COVID-19 treatment. While peptidomimetic inhibitors such as nirmatrelvir have demonstrated clinical efficacy, their effects on M pro quaternary structure dynamics remain poorly understood. Emerging evidence suggests these compounds may paradoxically promote dimerization, a phenomenon termed ligand-induced dimerization (LID), which could enhance catalytic activity at low inhibitor concentrations. Here, we employed an integrated mass spectrometry platform combining metabolic 15 N labeling with native mass spectrometry (nMS), hydrogen-deuterium exchange mass spectrometry (HDX-MS), and molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to elucidate the dynamic nature of M pro dimerization and its modulation by peptidomimetic inhibitors. We demonstrate that apo-M pro exists in a dynamic monomer-dimer equilibrium characterized by rapid subunit exchange. Remarkably, peptidomimetic inhibitors, including nirmatrelvir, GC376, and MPI8, kinetically trap the dimeric state, suppressing subunit exchange and stabilizing the dimeric form of M pro . Region-resolved HDX-MS revealed that inhibitor binding induces widespread conformational stabilization extending beyond the active site to the N-finger (residues 1-17) and dimer interface (residues 291-302). A notable transition of exchange mode from bimodal (EX1) to unimodal (EX2) in the N-finger region provided a sensitive structural marker of ligand-induced dimerization. Molecular dynamics simulations confirmed that the observed stabilization arises primarily from dimer formation rather than direct ligand contacts. These findings establish a mechanistic basis for inhibitor-stabilized dimerization, identify the N-finger as a dynamic reporter of quaternary structure transitions, and demonstrate the power of integrated mass spectrometry for dissecting complex protein assembly dynamics with therapeutic implications for antiviral drug development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the structural dynamics of SARS-CoV-2 main protease (M pro) and its response to peptidomimetic inhibitors, which are critical for understanding their therapeutic effects on viral polyprotein processing.
Design and population
The study employed an integrated mass spectrometry platform to analyze the dynamic nature of M pro dimerization and its modulation by peptidomimetic inhibitors, focusing on SARS-CoV-2 and its variants.
Core methods
The researchers used metabolic 15 N labeling, native mass spectrometry (nMS), hydrogen-deuterium exchange mass spectrometry (HDX-MS), and molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to examine the structural changes and dimerization dynamics of M pro in response to inhibitors.
Main results
The results showed that apo-M pro exists in a dynamic monomer-dimer equilibrium with rapid subunit exchange, and that peptidomimetic inhibitors, including nirmatrelvir, GC376, and MPI8, kinetically trap the dimeric state, suppressing subunit exchange and stabilizing the dimeric form of M pro.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish a mechanistic basis for inhibitor-stabilized dimerization and highlight the N-finger region as a dynamic reporter of quaternary structure transitions, offering new insights into the structural modulation of M pro by therapeutic agents.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide critical structural insights that can inform the development of more effective antiviral drugs targeting SARS-CoV-2 main protease, with potential implications for improving therapeutic strategies against COVID-19.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro analyses using mass spectrometry and simulations, and the evidence is based on abstract-level reporting, which may not fully capture in vivo dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate.

活动性严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 病毒体的生物安全 3 级冷冻电子断层扫描。

Biosafety level-3 cryo-electron tomography of active severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 virions.
作者： Hideo Fukuhara, Hisham M Dokainish, Shunsuke Kita, Koshiro Tabata, Akira Takasu, Juha T Huiskonen, Yuki Anraku, Tomo Nomai, Taiyo Someya, Toshiya Senda
摘要中文翻译了解病原病毒在其天然感染状态下的结构特性对于合理的 疫苗 和药物开发至关重要。然而,在生物安全 3 级 (BSL-3) 遏制下进行冷冻电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 面临着巨大的挑战。在这里,我们在 BSL-3 设施内建立并验证了高端冷冻电镜工作流程,全面集成了设施设计、密封措施、样品处理、冷冻转移系统和净化协议。为了证明该工作流程的实用性和可重复性,我们应用冷冻电子断层扫描技术来研究严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 的天然活性状态。我们的观察证实,未固定的病毒颗粒表现出与之前报道的固定或灭活制剂相当的多形性特征,证实这些结构特征代表天然特征而不是化学固定的伪影。此外,观察到表面尖峰的空间重新分布。这种强大的 BSL-3 冷冻电镜工作流程的建立为新出现的高后果病原体的结构表征提供了实用的蓝图,支持大流行防范、疫苗 开发和 抗病毒药物 发现。

Biosafety level-3 cryo-electron tomography of active severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 virions.

Authors: Hideo Fukuhara, Hisham M Dokainish, Shunsuke Kita, Koshiro Tabata, Akira Takasu, Juha T Huiskonen, Yuki Anraku, Tomo Nomai, Taiyo Someya, Toshiya Senda
Original abstract (English)
Understanding the structural properties of pathogenic viruses in their native, infectious state is critical for rational vaccine and drug development. However, conducting cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) under biosafety level 3 (BSL-3) containment presents significant challenges. Here, we established and validated a high-end cryo-EM workflow within a BSL-3 facility, comprehensively integrating facility design, containment measures, sample handling, cryo-transfer systems, and decontamination protocols. To demonstrate the utility and reproducibility of this workflow, we applied cryo-electron tomography to investigate severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 in its native, active state. Our observations confirm that unfixed viral particles display pleomorphic features comparable to those previously reported in fixed or inactivated preparations, validating that these structural characteristics represent native features rather than artifacts of chemical fixation. Furthermore, spatial redistribution of surface spikes was observed. The establishment of this robust BSL-3 cryo-EM workflow provides a practical blueprint for the structural characterization of emerging high-consequence pathogens, supporting pandemic preparedness, vaccine development, and antiviral discovery.

Methods to Account for Competing Events With Unknown Duration in Health Policy Evaluations: The Case of COVID-19.

Methods to Account for Competing Events With Unknown Duration in Health Policy Evaluations: The Case of COVID-19.
作者： Caleb W. Easterly, Justin G. Trogdon, G Mark Holmes, Marisa Elena Domino, Mark Holmes
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to compare methods for estimating policy effects in the presence of competing events such as COVID-19. The background highlights the need for robust analytical approaches to account for competing events with unknown durations in health policy evaluations.
设计与对象
A simulation study was conducted to evaluate the performance of single interrupted time series (ITS) and comparative interrupted time series (CITS) estimators when policy implementation occurred near a competing event, specifically COVID-19. The study included 2080 scenarios with varying durations of COVID-19 effects, time between policy implementation and COVID-19 onset, and magnitudes of policy effects.
核心方法
The methods involved creating simulated time series with 99 monthly observations from October 2015 to December 2023. Two ITS approaches were tested: one assuming different durations of COVID-19 effects (ITS-Assume) and another using grid search to find the best-fitting duration (ITS-Grid Search). Outcomes measured were percent bias, power, and Type 1 error.
主要结果
CITS had the lowest bias and Type 1 error and maintained high power across scenarios, except for intercept changes and when assumptions about shared COVID-19 effects between treatment and control groups were violated. ITS-Grid Search showed lower bias and Type 1 error than ITS-Assume, which produced biased estimates when assumptions were incorrect.
解释与创新
作者解释说,利用 CITS 等对照组的估计方法可以减少 ITS 框架中竞争事件的偏差。他们指出,ITS-网格搜索优于假设先验持续时间而不需要对照组的方法。
科研与公卫意义
The findings inform the cautious use of ITS methods in health policy evaluations involving competing events like COVID-19, emphasizing the importance of control groups and adaptive duration estimation to improve accuracy.
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对模拟数据的依赖以及政策效果和竞争事件可以量化的假设而受到限制。证据是温和的,因为它基于未经现实世界验证的模拟研究。

Methods to Account for Competing Events With Unknown Duration in Health Policy Evaluations: The Case of COVID-19.

Authors: Caleb W. Easterly, Justin G. Trogdon, G Mark Holmes, Marisa Elena Domino, Mark Holmes
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To compare methods for estimating policy effects in the presence of competing events such as COVID-19. STUDY SETTING AND DESIGN: We created a simulation study to evaluate how single interrupted time series (ITS) and comparative interrupted time series (CITS) estimators performed when policy implementation occurred near a competing event (COVID-19). The onset of the competing event was known (March 2020) but the duration was unknown. ITS approaches included assuming different COVID-19 durations (ITS-Assume) or searching for the COVID-19 duration that provided the best fit to the data (ITS-Grid Search). Outcomes included percent bias, power, and Type 1 error. DATA SOURCES AND ANALYTIC SAMPLE: We varied the duration of COVID-19 effects, the time between the policy implementation and COVID-19 onset, and the magnitude and sign of the policy effects relative to COVID-19 (2080 scenarios). Each simulated time series had 99 monthly observations (October 2015-December 2023). PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: CITS had the lowest bias and Type 1 error and had high power across scenarios, except for intercept changes and when assumptions about shared COVID-19 effects between treatment and control groups were violated. ITS-Grid Search had lower bias and Type 1 error than ITS-Assume, which produced biased parameter estimates when the assumptions were incorrect. Estimates were more accurate when the policy was implemented prior to the competing event. CONCLUSIONS: Using estimation approaches that leverage control groups, such as CITS, can mitigate the potential bias of competing events in ITS frameworks, but these approaches are biased when competing event effects differ between treatment and control groups. Searching for the best-fitting competing event duration does not require a control group and outperforms methods that assume the duration of competing event effects a priori. More generally, researchers should be cautious about bias, specification error, and low power when using ITS methods with competing events.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare methods for estimating policy effects in the presence of competing events such as COVID-19. The background highlights the need for robust analytical approaches to account for competing events with unknown durations in health policy evaluations.
Design and population
A simulation study was conducted to evaluate the performance of single interrupted time series (ITS) and comparative interrupted time series (CITS) estimators when policy implementation occurred near a competing event, specifically COVID-19. The study included 2080 scenarios with varying durations of COVID-19 effects, time between policy implementation and COVID-19 onset, and magnitudes of policy effects.
Core methods
The methods involved creating simulated time series with 99 monthly observations from October 2015 to December 2023. Two ITS approaches were tested: one assuming different durations of COVID-19 effects (ITS-Assume) and another using grid search to find the best-fitting duration (ITS-Grid Search). Outcomes measured were percent bias, power, and Type 1 error.
Main results
CITS had the lowest bias and Type 1 error and maintained high power across scenarios, except for intercept changes and when assumptions about shared COVID-19 effects between treatment and control groups were violated. ITS-Grid Search showed lower bias and Type 1 error than ITS-Assume, which produced biased estimates when assumptions were incorrect.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that estimation approaches leveraging control groups, like CITS, can reduce bias from competing events in ITS frameworks. They noted that ITS-Grid Search outperforms methods assuming a priori durations without requiring a control group.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the cautious use of ITS methods in health policy evaluations involving competing events like COVID-19, emphasizing the importance of control groups and adaptive duration estimation to improve accuracy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on simulated data and the assumption that policy effects and competing events can be quantified. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a simulation study without real-world validation.
