Epidemiology of influenza before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic in China: An observational study from Zhejiang, China (2010-2023).

Epidemiology of influenza before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic in China: An observational study from Zhejiang, China (2010-2023).
作者： Ye-Wen Chen, Jing-Cao Li, Fang-Zhi Luo, Feng Ling, Zhao Yu, Lauren Linde, Yang Li, Hui Huang, Andreas Handel, Benjamin John Cowling
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估中国的零新冠政策如何影响流感趋势,因为流感的 流行病学 由于大流行的社会限制而发生了变化,目前尚不清楚这些变化是否适用于中国。
设计与对象
这项观察性研究分析了 2010 年至 2023 年中国浙江省的每周 监测 数据,包括 2017 年至 2023 年期间 24,165 例病例的个人数据。该研究重点关注“零新冠”政策实施和放宽期间的流感诊断。
核心方法
研究人员应用变化点检测来识别流感趋势的结构性变化,并使用间断时间序列模型通过差异(O - P)和比率(P/O)比较预测(P)和观察(O)病例来量化政策的影响。对个体层面的数据进行了血统和人口变化的分析。
主要结果
在零新冠政策期间,2020年观察到的流感病例减少了248,781例(P/O:2.40;95%预测区间,2.28至2.52),2021年减少了204,619例(1.72;1.63至1.81),2022年减少了199,773例(1.66;1.58至1.81)。1.76)。2023 年政策放松后,病例数超出预期 148,662 例(0.8;0.76 至 0.84),但 5 岁以下儿童数量持续下降。B/Victoria谱系在大流行早期占主导地位(2021年占病例的99%以上),而到2023年,A/H3(55%)和A/H1(41%)流感成为最流行的毒株。
解释与创新
该研究解释说,中国的零新冠政策与流感传播动态的变化有关,放松后暴发的爆发性增长,除了幼儿之外。它强调了限制期间和之后血统优势的加剧,其长期影响尚不确定。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解公共卫生政策如何影响流感 传播 动态,并深入了解政策变化后未来 暴发 的潜力,特别是在不同年龄组。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对 监测 数据的依赖以及可能存在未测量的混杂因素而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于观察数据以及预测病例与观察病例的比较。

Epidemiology of influenza before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic in China: An observational study from Zhejiang, China (2010-2023).

Authors: Ye-Wen Chen, Jing-Cao Li, Fang-Zhi Luo, Feng Ling, Zhao Yu, Lauren Linde, Yang Li, Hui Huang, Andreas Handel, Benjamin John Cowling
Original abstract (English)
The epidemiology of influenza has shifted in recent years due to COVID-19 social restrictions.However, it remains unclear whether these findings apply to China, where a strict Zero-COVID policy was implemented.We evaluated influenza trends in Zhejiang Province, China from 2010-2023before, during, and after the strict policy.Specifically, using weekly surveillance data (2010-2023), we applied changepoint detection to identify structural shifts and interrupted time-series models to quantify policy impacts by comparing predicted (P) and observed (O) influenza cases based on differences (O -P) and ratios (P/O).Individual-level data (2017-2023; N=24,165 cases) were also analyzed for lineage and demographic shifts.A total of 4,295,302 influenza diagnoses occurred during the study period.During the policy, observed cases dropped by 248,781 cases in 2020 (P/O: 2.40; 95% prediction interval, 2.28 to 2.52), 204,619 in 2021 (1.72; 1.63 to 1.81), and 199,773 in 2022 (1.66; 1.58 to 1.76).Following policy relaxation in 2023, influenza cases exceeded expectations by 148,662 (0.8; 0.76 to 0.84); however, cases in children under 5 still declined by 22,388 (1.08; 1.04 to 1.12).B/Victoria lineage dominated early in the pandemic (>99% of cases in 2021), while influenza A/H3 (55%) and A/H1 (41%) emerged as the most prevalent strains by 2023.China's Zero-COVID policy was associated with changes in influenza transmission dynamics, with explosive outbreaks after relaxation except among young children.Changes in lineage predominance among lineages intensified during and after restrictions, with uncertain long-term implications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate how the Zero-COVID policy in China affected influenza trends, as the epidemiology of influenza has shifted due to social restrictions from the pandemic, and it remains unclear whether these changes apply to China.
Design and population
This observational study analyzed weekly surveillance data from Zhejiang Province, China, spanning 2010-2023, including individual-level data from 24,165 cases between 2017-2023. The study focused on influenza diagnoses during the implementation and relaxation of the Zero-COVID policy.
Core methods
The researchers applied changepoint detection to identify structural shifts in influenza trends and used interrupted time-series models to quantify the impact of the policy by comparing predicted (P) and observed (O) cases through differences (O - P) and ratios (P/O). Individual-level data were analyzed for lineage and demographic changes.
Main results
During the Zero-COVID policy, observed influenza cases dropped by 248,781 in 2020 (P/O: 2.40; 95% prediction interval, 2.28 to 2.52), 204,619 in 2021 (1.72; 1.63 to 1.81), and 199,773 in 2022 (1.66; 1.58 to 1.76). After policy relaxation in 2023, cases exceeded expectations by 148,662 (0.8; 0.76 to 0.84), but declines in children under 5 persisted. The B/Victoria lineage dominated early in the pandemic (>99% of cases in 2021), while influenza A/H3 (55%) and A/H1 (41%) became the most prevalent strains by 2023.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets that China's Zero-COVID policy was associated with changes in influenza transmission dynamics, with explosive outbreaks after relaxation except among young children. It highlights the intensification of lineage predominance during and after restrictions, with uncertain long-term implications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the understanding of how public health policies can influence influenza transmission dynamics and provide insights into the potential for future outbreaks following policy changes, particularly in different age groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on surveillance data and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on observational data and comparisons of predicted versus observed cases.

在一系列临床前治疗环境中研究抗 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 多聚体双环肽抑制剂

Investigation of anti-SARS-CoV-2 multimeric bicyclic peptide inhibitors in a range of preclinical therapeutic settings
作者： Maximilian A. J. Harman, Katherine U. Gaynor, Guido Papa, Simone Pellegrino, G. A. Bezerra, Brian McGuinness, Phil Jeffrey, Paul Beswick, Steven J. Stanway, Katerine Van Rietschoten
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 等呼吸道病毒的传播在过去 30 年中提出了重大挑战,迄今为止几乎没有有效的治疗选择。
设计与对象
本研究利用工具分子对多种临床前 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 挑战环境中的多价双环 (Bicycle) 分子功效进行更深入的评估。
核心方法
我们通过皮下和鼻内给药途径探索暴露前预防和暴露后治疗设置。
主要结果
令人欣慰的是,在所有研究中,我们都观察到了疗效,显着降低了每个研究终点的感染性病毒负荷。
解释与创新
这些数据进一步支持 Bicycle 分子作为大流行防范背景下差异化 抗病毒药物 治疗类别的候选资格。
科研与公卫意义
Bicycle 分子在每项研究中都表现出了积极的成果,并表明 Bicycle 分子作为方便有效的 抗病毒药物 治疗,可能是帮助未来应对新病毒大流行的重要补充。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Investigation of anti-SARS-CoV-2 multimeric bicyclic peptide inhibitors in a range of preclinical therapeutic settings

Authors: Maximilian A. J. Harman, Katherine U. Gaynor, Guido Papa, Simone Pellegrino, G. A. Bezerra, Brian McGuinness, Phil Jeffrey, Paul Beswick, Steven J. Stanway, Katerine Van Rietschoten
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The spread of respiratory viruses, such as influenza and SARS-CoV-2, has presented significant challenges over the last 30 years, with few effective therapeutic options available to this day. Bicyclic peptides represent a unique, modular modality in the antiviral armamentarium against future pandemics. This study utilizes tool molecules to provide a deeper evaluation of multivalent bicyclic (Bicycle) molecule efficacy in several preclinical SARS-CoV-2 challenge settings. We explore both pre-exposure prophylaxis and post-exposure therapeutic settings via subcutaneous and intranasal routes of administration. We contextualize this further in bespoke scenarios where the immune system is compromised, as well as viral transmission. Promisingly, in all studies, we observe efficacy, significantly reducing infectious viral burden at each study endpoint. These data further support the candidacy of Bicycle molecules as a differentiated antiviral therapeutic class in the context of pandemic preparedness. IMPORTANCE The COVID-19 pandemic triggered a rapid wave of innovation, accelerating the delivery of new vaccine technology and antiviral treatments. In our first paper, we described the discovery and molecular optimization of Bicycle molecules as a novel drug class for the potential treatment of SARS-CoV-2. Here, we have performed deeper characterization of these tool molecules in established animal models to evaluate scenarios for deployment, simulating SARS-CoV-2 transmission, testing more convenient delivery routes, such as intranasal, as well as looking at immunocompromised animals. The Bicycle molecules demonstrated positive outcomes in each of these studies and suggest that Bicycle molecules, as convenient and effective antiviral treatments, could be an important addition to help future preparedness against new viral pandemics.

Core methods

From Viral Detection to Disease Measurement: Lessons from COVID-19.

From Viral Detection to Disease Measurement: Lessons from COVID-19.
作者： Richard M. Fleming, M. Fleming, NRP BA BS Matthew R Fleming

问题与背景
The study aimed to highlight the distinction between detecting an infectious stimulus and measuring the disease it produces, as demonstrated by the COVID-19 pandemic.

该研究是对病毒感染与疾病之间关系的概念分析,重点是 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 及其相关的临床结果。

From Viral Detection to Disease Measurement: Lessons from COVID-19.

Authors: Richard M. Fleming, M. Fleming, NRP BA BS Matthew R Fleming
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic demonstrated a fundamental distinction between detecting an infectious stimulus and measuring the disease produced by that stimulus. SARS-CoV-2 establishes infection, whereas COVID-19 reflects the interaction among viral replication, host immunity, inflammation, endothelial dysfunction, coagulation and tissue injury. These processes are related but are not interchangeable measurements. A positive molecular test establishes evidence of viral genetic material; it does not, by itself, quantify tissue injury, systemic thromboinflammation or disease severity. Similarly, individual biomarkers measure components of a biological response rather than necessarily defining the complete disease state. Clinical outcomes, including hospitalization and death, provide important measures of consequence but do not necessarily identify the mechanisms that produced them. COVID-19 therefore provides a case study in measurement fidelity: the quantity measured must be distinguished from the biological process that the investigator seeks to understand. Viral evolution, changing population immunity, vaccination, immune imprinting and human behaviour further demonstrate that the relationship between infection and disease changes over time. Future pandemic preparedness should integrate pathogen detection with mechanistically appropriate measurements of host response, tissue injury and clinical outcome, while maintaining explicit boundaries between measurement, inference and causation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to highlight the distinction between detecting an infectious stimulus and measuring the disease it produces, as demonstrated by the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
The study is a conceptual analysis of the relationship between viral infection and disease, focusing on SARS-CoV-2 and its associated clinical outcomes.
Core methods
The methods involve a review and synthesis of existing knowledge on viral detection, disease measurement, and the factors influencing their relationship, including viral evolution, immunity, and human behavior.
Main results
The results emphasize that viral genetic material detection does not equate to quantifying tissue injury, systemic thromboinflammation, or disease severity, and that individual biomarkers reflect components of a biological response rather than the complete disease state.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the pandemic has shown the need to distinguish between infection and disease, and that this distinction is crucial for accurate measurement and understanding of the biological processes involved.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of integrating pathogen detection with appropriate measurements of host response, tissue injury, and clinical outcomes to improve pandemic preparedness and public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to conceptual analysis and does not provide empirical data, but it offers a high-level framework for understanding the relationship between infection and disease.

Host-Directed p53 and NRF2 Co-Activation Reverses Acute COVID-19 and Long COVID Pathology by Restoring the miR-29 and miR-145 Epigenetic Axis

Host-Directed p53 and NRF2 Co-Activation Reverses Acute COVID-19 and Long COVID Pathology by Restoring the miR-29 and miR-145 Epigenetic Axis
作者： Boominathan Lakshmanane
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to address the lack of phase-independent mechanistic therapies for COVID-19 and Long COVID by proposing a host-directed therapeutic framework targeting p53 and NRF2 co-activation.
设计与对象
该研究涉及计算机建模和交叉研究综合,以评估在 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的急性期和急性期后共同激活 p53 和 NRF2 的治疗潜力。
核心方法
该研究使用从已发表的周转动力学参数化的刚性常微分方程 (ODE) 模拟 (solve_ivp/BDF) 来模拟 p53/NRF2 共激活对 miR-29 和 miR-145 动力学及其下游效应子的影响。
主要结果
模拟显示,p53/NRF2 共激活在 Uth = 0.4–0.6 激动剂阈值附近诱导急剧分叉转换,导致 miR-29 恢复 13.75 倍,miR-145 恢复 54.39 倍,NF-κB 抑制 94.0%,同时 IFN-γ、TF、Sema3a 和 GzmK 减少锁定 PASC 稳态时为 98.3% 至 99.1%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,共同激活 p53 和 NRF2 可以通过恢复 miR-29 和 miR-145 表观遗传轴来逆转 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的病理效应,为感染的急性期和急性期后提供一种新的治疗策略。
科研与公卫意义
These findings suggest a potential new approach for treating both acute and long-term sequelae of SARS-CoV-2 infection, which could inform future research and therapeutic development for COVID-19 and related conditions.
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖计算机模型和交叉研究综合而受到限制,关键警告包括 miR-29 病毒位点提取不完整、miR-145 验证的跨物种差异以及缺乏前瞻性随机试验,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Host-Directed p53 and NRF2 Co-Activation Reverses Acute COVID-19 and Long COVID Pathology by Restoring the miR-29 and miR-145 Epigenetic Axis

Authors: Boominathan Lakshmanane
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Despite over 524,592 COVID-19 papers indexed in PubMed as documented in our synthesis compilation and widespread vaccine deployment over the past six years, COVID-19 has caused more than 7.11 million officially reported deaths globally—with WHO excess mortality estimates reaching 14.83 million for 2020–2021 alone and cumulative excess death estimates spanning 18.5 to 35.2 million [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]—while leaving an estimated 400 million individuals worldwide with Long COVID (post-acute sequelae of SARS-CoV-2, or PASC) at an approximate $1 trillion annual economic cost [1, 5]. To address the absence of a phase-independent mechanistic therapy, we propose and computationally evaluate a unified host-directed therapeutic framework centered on co-activating the cellular stress-response regulators p53 and NRF2 [1]. Across acute and post-acute phases, SARS-CoV-2 suppresses p53 and NRF2 signaling, depleting miR-29 (which harbors 11 conserved binding sites on the SARS-CoV-2 genome and directly targets IFNG) and miR-145 (which restrains Tissue Factor F3, Semaphorin-3A Sema3a, Granzyme K GZMK, and MYC) while hyperactivating NF-κB [1, 7, 8, 9, 10]. This upstream collapse drives persistent elevations in IFN-γ, Tissue Factor (TF), Sema3a, and Granzyme K (GzmK), promoting myocarditis, thromboembolism, myocardial dysfunction, and age-related tissue remodeling independently of age-associated immune dysfunction [1, 11, 12, 13]. Stiff ordinary differential equation (ODE) simulations (solve_ivp/BDF) parameterized from published turnover kinetics demonstrate that simulated p53/NRF2 co-activation induces a sharp bifurcation switch near an agonist threshold of Uth = 0.4–0.6 under persistent antigenic stimulation, producing a 13.75-fold recovery of miR-29 and a 54.39-fold recovery of miR-145 while suppressing NF-κB by 94.0% and reducing IFN-γ, TF, Sema3a, and GzmK by 98.3% to 99.1% in locked PASC steady states [14]. In an aggressive acute infection simulation (rv = 0.12 h-1), therapy initiated at 24 h lowers 168 h viral load by 46.2% and suppresses the four terminal effectors by 92.0% to 95.1% [14]. Because these quantitative dynamics derive from in silico modeling and cross-study synthesis, key evidentiary caveats remain—including incomplete extraction of individual duplex coordinates for all 11 miR-29 viral sites, cross-species differences in miR-145 validation for GZMK and Sema3a, compartment-specific p53 paradoxes, and the absence of prospective randomized trials testing combined p53/NRF2 agonism in PASC [1, 10, 15].
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of phase-independent mechanistic therapies for COVID-19 and Long COVID by proposing a host-directed therapeutic framework targeting p53 and NRF2 co-activation.
Design and population
The research involved in silico modeling and cross-study synthesis to evaluate the therapeutic potential of co-activating p53 and NRF2 across both acute and post-acute phases of SARS-CoV-2 infection.
Core methods
The study used stiff ordinary differential equation (ODE) simulations (solve_ivp/BDF) parameterized from published turnover kinetics to model the effects of p53/NRF2 co-activation on miR-29 and miR-145 dynamics and their downstream effectors.
Main results
Simulations showed that p53/NRF2 co-activation induced a sharp bifurcation switch near an agonist threshold of Uth = 0.4–0.6, resulting in a 13.75-fold recovery of miR-29, a 54.39-fold recovery of miR-145, and a 94.0% suppression of NF-κB, with reductions in IFN-γ, TF, Sema3a, and GzmK by 98.3% to 99.1% in locked PASC steady states.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that co-activating p53 and NRF2 can reverse the pathological effects of SARS-CoV-2 by restoring the miR-29 and miR-145 epigenetic axis, offering a novel therapeutic strategy for both acute and post-acute phases of infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest a potential new approach for treating both acute and long-term sequelae of SARS-CoV-2 infection, which could inform future research and therapeutic development for COVID-19 and related conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in silico modeling and cross-study synthesis, with key caveats including incomplete extraction of miR-29 viral sites, cross-species differences in miR-145 validation, and the absence of prospective randomized trials, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

Comparison of methodological approaches in COVID-19 vaccine effectiveness estimation using observational data

Comparison of methodological approaches in COVID-19 vaccine effectiveness estimation using observational data
作者： Huiberts AJ, Smagge B, Werkhoven Hv, de Gier B, Monge S, de Melker HE, Hahné SJ, Grobbee DE, de Wijgert JHv, Hof Svd
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:目标试验仿真(TTE)是一个框架,用于系统地解决使用观察数据时因果推理中潜在的设计相关偏差。我们使用四种 TTE 方法估计了 2023 年 10 月 2 日至 2024 年 4 月 2 日期间 Omicron XBB.1.5 加强剂 疫苗接种 对抗 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的 疫苗 有效性 (VE)。方法:设计了一项假设的目标试验,其中合格的参与者将被随机分配接受或不接受加强 疫苗接种。使用荷兰正在进行的前瞻性 队列研究 研究的数据,使用四种方法来模拟这一假设试验。第一种和第二种方法将零时间定义为增强器 疫苗接种 推出的开始,并将 疫苗接种 视为随时间变化的变量。第一种方法通过回归调整来调整混杂因素,而第二种方法则使用逆概率加权。第三种和第四种方法使用多个时间零。在第三种方法中,所有在特定日期接受加强疫苗 疫苗接种 的人都与当天(尚未)接种疫苗的人进行 1:1 匹配。在第四种方法中,使用逆概率加权调整混杂因素。结果:第一种、第二种、第三种和第四种方法的总体 VE 分别为 30% (95%CI:26-34)、28% (24-32)、25% (18-30) 和 27% (24-31)。VE 随着 疫苗接种 时间的增加而减少。结论:四种方法的估计 VE 相似。方法的选择应基于模型假设、兴趣估计和可行性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较使用观察数据估计 疫苗 针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的有效性 (VE) 的不同方法,解决因果推理中与设计相关的偏差的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究使用了荷兰正在进行的一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究的观察数据,估计了 2023 年 10 月 2 日至 2024 年 4 月 2 日期间 Omicron XBB.1.5 加强剂 疫苗接种 针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的 VE。
核心方法
设计了一项假设的目标试验,参与者被随机分配接受或不接受加强剂 疫苗接种,并使用四种方法使用 队列研究 数据来模拟该试验。
主要结果
第一种、第二种、第三种和第四种方法的总体 VE 分别为 30% (95%CI:26-34)、28% (24-32)、25% (18-30) 和 27% (24-31)。VE 随着 疫苗接种 时间的增加而减少。
解释与创新
作者解释说,四种方法的估计 VE 相似,强调了根据模型假设、兴趣估计和可行性选择方法的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为观察性研究中估计 疫苗 有效性的方法学方法的选择提供了信息,这对于公共卫生决策和 疫苗 政策至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且估计的 VE 值基于假设的试验模拟,可能无法完全捕捉现实世界的复杂性。证据强度中等。

Comparison of methodological approaches in COVID-19 vaccine effectiveness estimation using observational data

Authors: Huiberts AJ, Smagge B, Werkhoven Hv, de Gier B, Monge S, de Melker HE, Hahné SJ, Grobbee DE, de Wijgert JHv, Hof Svd
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Target trial emulation (TTE) is a framework to systematically address potential design-related biases in causal inference when using observational data. We estimated vaccine effectiveness (VE) of the Omicron XBB.1.5 booster vaccination against SARS-CoV-2 infection between 2 October 2023 and 2 April 2024 using four TTE approaches. Methods: A hypothetical target trial was designed where eligible participants would be randomly assigned to receive booster vaccination or not. Four approaches were used to emulate this hypothetical trial using data of an ongoing prospective cohort study in the Netherlands. The first and second approach defined time zero as the start of the booster vaccination rollout and considered vaccination as a time-varying variable. The first approach adjusted for confounders by regression adjustment, while the second used inverse probability weighting. The third and fourth approach used multiple time zeros. In the third approach, all persons who received a booster vaccination on a specific day were matched 1:1 to persons who were not (yet) vaccinated on that day. In the fourth approach, confounders were adjusted for using inverse probability weighting. Results: Overall VE was 30% (95%CI:26-34), 28% (24-32), 25% (18-30) and 27% (24-31) in the first, second, third and fourth approach, respectively. VE decreased as time since vaccination increased. Conclusions: Estimated VE was similar across the four approaches. The choice of approach should be based on the model assumptions, estimand of interest, and feasibility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare different methodological approaches for estimating vaccine effectiveness (VE) against SARS-CoV-2 infection using observational data, addressing the challenge of design-related biases in causal inference.
Design and population
The study used observational data from an ongoing prospective cohort study in the Netherlands, estimating VE of the Omicron XBB.1.5 booster vaccination against SARS-CoV-2 infection between 2 October 2023 and 2 April 2024.
Core methods
A hypothetical target trial was designed where participants were randomly assigned to receive booster vaccination or not, and four approaches were used to emulate this trial using the cohort data.
Main results
Overall VE was 30% (95%CI:26-34), 28% (24-32), 25% (18-30) and 27% (24-31) in the first, second, third and fourth approach, respectively. VE decreased as time since vaccination increased.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the estimated VE was similar across the four approaches, highlighting the importance of selecting an approach based on model assumptions, estimand of interest, and feasibility.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the choice of methodological approaches for estimating vaccine effectiveness in observational studies, which is critical for public health decision-making and vaccine policy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the estimated VE values are based on hypothetical trial emulation, which may not fully capture real-world complexities. The evidence strength is moderate.

Detection of SARS-CoV-2 in Semen Months After COVID-19: Potential Evidence of Low-Level Replicative Persistence.

Detection of SARS-CoV-2 in Semen Months After COVID-19: Potential Evidence of Low-Level Replicative Persistence.
作者： Marcelo H Furtado, Fernanda R Souza, Lidia M Andrade, Bárbara P Mendes, Juan C González, Thiago P Furtado, Anisleidy Pérez Castillo, Samyra M S N Lacerda, Renato S Aguiar, Guilherme M J Costa
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
男性生殖道 (MGT),特别是睾丸,是一个免疫豁免部位,可能充当 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的病毒 储存宿主。
设计与对象
MATERIAL AND METHODS: This observational, prospective, cross-sectional study included 38 men treated at Mater Dei Hospital, Brazil, between October 2020 and October 2021, who had recovered from mild to moderate COVID-19 and consented to participate.
核心方法
精液样本是在症状出现后约 1 个月(15-45 天)、3 个月和 6 个月从症状轻微的参与者身上采集的。
主要结果
通过 RT-qPCR 在 38 名患者中的 21 名 (55%) 中检测到 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2,感染后 6 个月以上观察到高循环阈值 (Ct 38-39)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,MGT 可能充当病毒 储存宿主,强调了纵向精液分析对于监测临床恢复后病毒持续性和 储存宿主 动态的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究提供了病毒 RNA 和培养源信号长期持续存在的证据,表明精液中的复制活动受到限制或低水平。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Detection of SARS-CoV-2 in Semen Months After COVID-19: Potential Evidence of Low-Level Replicative Persistence.

Authors: Marcelo H Furtado, Fernanda R Souza, Lidia M Andrade, Bárbara P Mendes, Juan C González, Thiago P Furtado, Anisleidy Pérez Castillo, Samyra M S N Lacerda, Renato S Aguiar, Guilherme M J Costa
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Viral persistence is considered one of the key mechanisms driving long COVID symptoms. The male genital tract (MGT), particularly the testis, is an immune-privileged site that may serve as a viral reservoir for SARS-CoV-2. However, the presence of SARS-CoV-2 in semen and its long-term infectiousness remains controversial. OBJECTIVES: We aimed to investigate the long-term presence and potential replicative activity of SARS-CoV-2 in semen, evaluating whether the virus can persist and maintain low-level culture signals in the MGT well beyond the acute phase of infection. MATERIAL AND METHODS: This observational, prospective, cross-sectional study included 38 men treated at Mater Dei Hospital, Brazil, between October 2020 and October 2021, who had recovered from mild to moderate COVID-19 and consented to participate. Semen samples were collected from mildly symptomatic participants at approximately 1 month (15- 45 days), 3 months, and 6 months after symptom onset. The moderately symptomatic group provided a single sample approximately 6 months post-infection. Semen analysis and SARS-CoV-2 detection were performed using RT-qPCR, followed by viral isolation in Vero CCL-81 cells. Potential viral persistence and replication signals were evaluated through immunofluorescence and transmission electron microscopy (TEM). RESULTS: SARS-CoV-2 was detected by RT-qPCR in 21 of 38 patients (55%), with high cycle threshold (Ct 38-39) values observed for more than 6 months post-infection. Following inoculation into Vero cell cultures, a low-level in vitro expansion of viral copies was identified in a subset of samples from mild and moderate patients. The potential presence of the virus was further supported by the presence of viral proteins via immunofluorescence and the identification of structures morphologically compatible with coronavirus-like particles by TEM. CONCLUSION: This study provides evidence of the long-term persistence of viral RNA and culture-derived signals suggestive of restricted or low-level replicative activity in semen. These findings suggest that the MGT may serve as a viral reservoir, highlighting the importance of longitudinal semen analysis for monitoring viral persistence and reservoir dynamics after clinical recovery.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The male genital tract (MGT), particularly the testis, is an immune-privileged site that may serve as a viral reservoir for SARS-CoV-2.
Design and population
MATERIAL AND METHODS: This observational, prospective, cross-sectional study included 38 men treated at Mater Dei Hospital, Brazil, between October 2020 and October 2021, who had recovered from mild to moderate COVID-19 and consented to participate.

通过 DIA-ECCR 天然质谱分析完整 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 尖峰变体的糖型分布和硫酸乙酰肝素结合

Glycoform Distributions and Heparan Sulfate Binding of Intact SARS-CoV-2 Spike Variants by DIA-ECCR Native Mass Spectrometry
作者： Zhixin Xu, Chen Du, Eduardo Olmedillas, Erica Ollmann Saphire, Vicki Hopper Wysocki
摘要中文翻译糖基化影响 SARS CoV 2 刺突蛋白 (S) 的结构、免疫原性和进化选择,但其广泛的异质性阻碍了完整糖型分布的表征。在这里,我们展示了一种独立于数据的分析 - 电子捕获电荷还原 (DIA-ECCR) 原生质谱工作流程,用于直接分析多个变体的完整刺突糖型。DIA-ECCR 解决了糖型分布和较高质量亚群中的变异依赖性差异。基于已发布的位点特异性自下而上糖蛋白质组数据的蒙特卡罗模拟再现了 D614G 实验分布的中心质量,并支持完整测量和自下而上测量之间的联系,而更广泛的实验分布和第二个 Gamma 亚群表明模拟未捕获的异质性。质量光度测定和电荷检测质谱提供了与 DIA-ECCR 结果一致的正交测量。DIA-ECCR 还解决了与截短的硫酸乙酰肝素结合相关的质量变化,证明了异质蛋白质:配体复合物的分析。总之,这些结果概述了一个实用的工作流程,将 DIA-ECCR 与天然质谱 (nMS) 相结合,用于表征高度异质性糖蛋白组装体中完整的糖型分布和配体结合,并在抗原表征、疫苗 设计和生物制药质量控制方面具有潜在的应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决表征 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白 中糖型分布的广泛异质性的挑战,这阻碍了对其结构、免疫原性和进化选择的理解。
设计与对象
该研究引入了数据独立分析 - 电子捕获电荷还原 (DIA-ECCR) 原生质谱工作流程,可直接分析多个 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的完整刺突糖型。
核心方法
该研究利用 DIA-ECCR 天然质谱法来解决糖型分布和较高质量亚群中变异依赖性的差异,并使用基于位点特异性自下而上糖蛋白质组数据的蒙特卡罗模拟来支持研究结果。
主要结果
蒙特卡洛模拟再现了 D614G 实验分布的中心质量,并支持完整测量和自下而上测量之间的联系,而更广泛的实验分布和第二个伽马亚群表明模拟未捕获的异质性。
解释与创新
结果证明了 DIA-ECCR 在分析异质蛋白:配体复合物(例如用截短的硫酸乙酰肝素形成的复合物)方面的实用性,并概述了表征糖型分布和配体结合的实用工作流程。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现在抗原表征、疫苗 设计和生物制药质量控制方面具有潜在应用,为了解 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白 的结构和功能多样性提供了见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认蒙特卡罗模拟可能无法完全捕捉到实验分布中观察到的异质性,这表明需要使用其他方法进一步验证以加强证据。

Glycoform Distributions and Heparan Sulfate Binding of Intact SARS-CoV-2 Spike Variants by DIA-ECCR Native Mass Spectrometry

Authors: Zhixin Xu, Chen Du, Eduardo Olmedillas, Erica Ollmann Saphire, Vicki Hopper Wysocki
Original abstract (English)
Glycosylation influences the structure, immunogenicity, and evolutionary selection of the SARS CoV 2 spike protein (S), but its extensive heterogeneity has hindered characterization of intact glycoform distributions. Here, we illustrate a data-independent analysis - electron capture charge reduction (DIA-ECCR) native mass spectrometry workflow for direct analysis of intact spike glycoforms across multiple variants. DIA-ECCR resolves variant-dependent differences in glycoform distributions and higher mass subpopulations. Monte Carlo simulations based on published site-specific bottom-up glycoproteomic data reproduce the central mass of the D614G experimental distribution and support a connection between intact and bottom-up measurements, while the broader experimental distributions and a second Gamma subpopulation indicate heterogeneity not captured by the simulations Mass photometry and charge detection mass spectrometry provide orthogonal measurements consistent with the DIA-ECCR results. DIA-ECCR also resolves mass shifts associated with binding of truncated heparan sulfate, demonstrating analysis of a heterogeneous protein:ligand complex. Together, these results outline a practical workflow that combines DIA-ECCR with native mass spectrometry (nMS) for characterizing intact glycoform distributions and ligand binding in highly heterogeneous glycoprotein assemblies, with potential applications in antigen characterization, vaccine design, and biopharmaceutical quality control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges in characterizing the extensive heterogeneity of glycoform distributions in the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein, which has hindered understanding of its structure, immunogenicity, and evolutionary selection.
Design and population
The research introduced a data-independent analysis - electron capture charge reduction (DIA-ECCR) native mass spectrometry workflow to directly analyze intact spike glycoforms across multiple SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Core methods
The study utilized DIA-ECCR native mass spectrometry to resolve variant-dependent differences in glycoform distributions and higher mass subpopulations, with Monte Carlo simulations based on site-specific bottom-up glycoproteomic data used to support the findings.
Main results
Monte Carlo simulations reproduced the central mass of the D614G experimental distribution and supported a connection between intact and bottom-up measurements, while broader experimental distributions and a second Gamma subpopulation indicated heterogeneity not captured by the simulations.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate the utility of DIA-ECCR in analyzing heterogeneous protein:ligand complexes, such as those formed with truncated heparan sulfate, and outline a practical workflow for characterizing glycoform distributions and ligand binding.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings have potential applications in antigen characterization, vaccine design, and biopharmaceutical quality control, offering insights into the structural and functional diversity of SARS-CoV-2 spike proteins.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that Monte Carlo simulations may not fully capture the heterogeneity observed in experimental distributions, suggesting that further validation with additional methods is needed to strengthen the evidence.

Post-COVID-19 Emergence and Spread of Hypertransmissible Cryptosporidium hominis gp60 Subtype IfA12G1R5, Spain.

Post-COVID-19 Emergence and Spread of Hypertransmissible Cryptosporidium hominis gp60 Subtype IfA12G1R5, Spain.
作者： Lidia Goterris, Pamela C Köster, Julia Fuentes, Elena Sulleiro, Marina Alguacil-Guillén, Miriam J Álvarez-Martínez, Alba Llop, Gema Fernández-Rivas, Anabel Fernández-Navarro, Isabel Fradejas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to investigate how the COVID-19 pandemic affected the transmission dynamics of Cryptosporidium hominis in Spain, particularly focusing on the emergence of hypertransmissible gp60 subtypes.
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2020 年大流行期间和之后西班牙的人型隐孢子虫 传播 模式,注意到 IfA12G1R5 等高传播亚型的迅速出现和传播。
核心方法
该研究调查了大流行遏制措施对人型隐孢子虫 传播 的影响,强调了这些措施如何选择性地改变人为 传播 并导致高传播亚型的出现。
主要结果
研究观察到,与人畜共患的艰难梭菌相对稳定的 传播 相比,高度传播的人类适应 gp60 亚型(例如 IfA12G1R5)的出现和传播更为明显。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为大流行遏制措施影响人型隐孢子虫 传播 动态的证据,导致高传播亚型占主导地位。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,与大流行相关的公共卫生干预措施可能间接影响了人隐孢子虫的 流行病学,这对 监测 和对人为 传播 模式的理解具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这限制了分析的深度和结论的可信度。

Post-COVID-19 Emergence and Spread of Hypertransmissible Cryptosporidium hominis gp60 Subtype IfA12G1R5, Spain.

Authors: Lidia Goterris, Pamela C Köster, Julia Fuentes, Elena Sulleiro, Marina Alguacil-Guillén, Miriam J Álvarez-Martínez, Alba Llop, Gema Fernández-Rivas, Anabel Fernández-Navarro, Isabel Fradejas
Original abstract (English)
COVID-19 pandemic containment measures in 2020 selectively altered established Cryptosporidium hominis anthroponotic transmission in Spain. Rapid emergence and expansion of hypertransmissible human-adapted gp60 subtypes such as IfA12G1R5 and, less markedly, IbA13G3 followed. Conversely, transmission of the largely zoonotic C. parvum remained comparatively stable, yielding a dual epidemiologic scenario in this country.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how the COVID-19 pandemic affected the transmission dynamics of Cryptosporidium hominis in Spain, particularly focusing on the emergence of hypertransmissible gp60 subtypes.
Design and population
The study analyzed Cryptosporidium hominis transmission patterns in Spain during and after the 2020 pandemic, noting the rapid emergence and spread of hypertransmissible subtypes such as IfA12G1R5.
Core methods
The study examined the impact of pandemic containment measures on Cryptosporidium hominis transmission, highlighting how these measures selectively altered anthroponotic transmission and led to the emergence of hypertransmissible subtypes.
Main results
The study observed that the emergence and spread of hypertransmissible human-adapted gp60 subtypes, such as IfA12G1R5, were more pronounced compared to the relatively stable transmission of the largely zoonotic C.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence of pandemic containment measures influencing the transmission dynamics of Cryptosporidium hominis, leading to the dominance of hypertransmissible subtypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that pandemic-related public health interventions may have indirectly influenced the epidemiology of Cryptosporidium hominis, with implications for surveillance and understanding of anthroponotic transmission patterns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which limits the depth of analysis and the confidence in its conclusions.

社区 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 活动无法解释加拿大安大略省不断上升的结核病报告:一项基于人群的时间序列研究

Rising Tuberculosis Presentations in Ontario, Canada Are Not Explained by Community SARS-CoV-2 Activity: A Population-Based Time-Series Study
作者： Natalie J. Wilson, Ashleigh R. Tuite, Clara Eunyoung Lee, Rachel J. Berkowitz, Benjamin Wiebe, Jade Hall, Dagmara Boushy, David N. Fisman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自 2020 年以来,几个高收入国家的结核病 (TB) 通报有所增加。提出的解释包括与流行病相关的 治疗 潜在结核病破坏、人口构成变化以及 SARS CoV 2 感染后的免疫功能障碍。
设计与对象
We conducted a population-based ecological time-series study of first TB- and nontuberculous mycobacterial (NTM)-coded hospital, emergency-department and ambulatory clinic presentations in the Greater Toronto/Hamilton Area Plus (GTHA+), Ontario (2024 population approximately 11.2 million), from April 2011 through March 2024.
核心方法
NTM 可以作为比较对象,因为它与 TB 具有相同的诊断途径,但在流行病学上有所不同。负二项式投影和全系列模型在三种替代趋势假设下估计了大流行时代的变化。分布式滞后非线性模型估计了 0 至 12 周内标准化社区病毒活动的累积率比;敏感性分析将最大滞后期延长至 26 周和 52 周。
主要结果
We identified 3,985 first TB-coded and 5,214 first NTM-coded acute-care presentations. Across six models, estimated pandemic-era TB increases ranged from 3% to 22%. No model suggested an increased in NTM. SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with TB (cumulative rate ratio per 1-SD increase over 0 to 12 weeks 0.909, 95% CI 0.822 to 1.006) or NTM (0.953, 0.868 to 1.047). No positive associations were identified in subgroup analyses or with maximum lags of 26 or 52 weeks.
解释与创新
我们发现,加拿大安大略省在大流行期间,结核病编码的急症护理报告略有增加,尽管其估计规模取决于对基本趋势的假设。NTM 编码的演示文稿没有增加。
科研与公卫意义
社区 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 活动与随后长达一年的结核病或 NTM 表现没有正相关,并且没有为人群水平结核病的增加提供时间解释。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告个体层面的影响,但该研究的结果是基于生态数据和有关潜在趋势的假设,这可能会限制结果的可信度。

Rising Tuberculosis Presentations in Ontario, Canada Are Not Explained by Community SARS-CoV-2 Activity: A Population-Based Time-Series Study

Authors: Natalie J. Wilson, Ashleigh R. Tuite, Clara Eunyoung Lee, Rachel J. Berkowitz, Benjamin Wiebe, Jade Hall, Dagmara Boushy, David N. Fisman
Original abstract (English)
Background: Tuberculosis (TB) notifications have increased in several high-income countries since 2020. Proposed explanations include pandemic related disruption of treatment for latent tuberculosis, changes in population composition, and immune dysfunction following SARS CoV 2 infection. We assessed whether TB presentations increased during the pandemic era and then tested whether community SARS CoV 2 activity was followed by increased mycobacterial disease. Methods: We conducted a population-based ecological time-series study of first TB- and nontuberculous mycobacterial (NTM)-coded hospital, emergency-department and ambulatory clinic presentations in the Greater Toronto/Hamilton Area Plus (GTHA+), Ontario (2024 population approximately 11.2 million), from April 2011 through March 2024. NTM served as a comparator because it shares diagnostic pathways with TB but differs epidemiologically. Negative binomial projection and full-series models estimated pandemic-era changes under three alternative trend assumptions. Distributed lag non linear models estimated cumulative rate ratios for standardized community viral activity over 0 to 12 weeks; sensitivity analyses extended the maximum lag to 26 and 52 weeks. Influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) were prespecified comparators; data for five additional viral exposures were obtained after the primary analysis was completed and analysed as exploratory comparisons. Results: We identified 3,985 first TB-coded and 5,214 first NTM-coded acute-care presentations. Across six models, estimated pandemic-era TB increases ranged from 3% to 22%. No model suggested an increased in NTM. SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with TB (cumulative rate ratio per 1-SD increase over 0 to 12 weeks 0.909, 95% CI 0.822 to 1.006) or NTM (0.953, 0.868 to 1.047). No positive associations were identified in subgroup analyses or with maximum lags of 26 or 52 weeks. Influenza was not associated with either outcome. RSV was associated with TB and NTM; only the RSV/NTM association met the Bonferroni threshold among the prespecified comparisons, and no exploratory associations met the experiment wide threshold. Conclusions: We identified a modest pandemic era increase in TB-coded acute-care presentations in Ontario, Canada, although its estimated size depended on assumptions about underlying trends. NTM-coded presentations did not increase. Community SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with subsequent TB or NTM presentations at lags of up to one year and did not provide a temporal explanation for the population-level TB increase. Individual-level effects cannot be excluded.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Tuberculosis (TB) notifications have increased in several high-income countries since 2020. Proposed explanations include pandemic related disruption of treatment for latent tuberculosis, changes in population composition, and immune dysfunction following SARS CoV 2 infection.
Design and population
We conducted a population-based ecological time-series study of first TB- and nontuberculous mycobacterial (NTM)-coded hospital, emergency-department and ambulatory clinic presentations in the Greater Toronto/Hamilton Area Plus (GTHA+), Ontario (2024 population approximately 11.2 million), from April 2011 through March 2024.
Core methods
NTM served as a comparator because it shares diagnostic pathways with TB but differs epidemiologically. Negative binomial projection and full-series models estimated pandemic-era changes under three alternative trend assumptions. Distributed lag non linear models estimated cumulative rate ratios for standardized community viral activity over 0 to 12 weeks; sensitivity analyses extended the maximum lag to 26 and 52 weeks.
Main results
We identified 3,985 first TB-coded and 5,214 first NTM-coded acute-care presentations. Across six models, estimated pandemic-era TB increases ranged from 3% to 22%. No model suggested an increased in NTM. SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with TB (cumulative rate ratio per 1-SD increase over 0 to 12 weeks 0.909, 95% CI 0.822 to 1.006) or NTM (0.953, 0.868 to 1.047). No positive associations were identified in subgroup analyses or with maximum lags of 26 or 52 weeks.
Interpretation and novelty
We identified a modest pandemic era increase in TB-coded acute-care presentations in Ontario, Canada, although its estimated size depended on assumptions about underlying trends. NTM-coded presentations did not increase.
Scientific and public-health significance
Community SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with subsequent TB or NTM presentations at lags of up to one year and did not provide a temporal explanation for the population-level TB increase.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report individual-level effects, but the study's findings are based on ecological data and assumptions about underlying trends, which may limit the confidence in the results.

应用人鼻粘膜离体培养系统模拟炎症反应

Application of a Human Nasal Mucosa Ex Vivo Culture System to Model Inflammatory Responses
作者： J. Lai, Hao-Ran Peng, Shi-Can Zhou, Chun-Yan Yao, Zhi-Li Zhang, Kai C. Fan, Ya-Wen Gao, Ling Jin, Zheng-Liang Gao, Ping Zhao
摘要中文翻译简介 鼻腔是上呼吸道的入口点,也是吸入病原体炎症反应的主要部位。方法从接受内窥镜鼻手术的患者身上获取人鼻粘膜(HNM)组织。来自房尘螨 (HDM) 过敏性鼻炎患者的 6 个 HNM 标本用于配对 Der p 1 攻击实验,8 个慢性鼻窦炎衍生的 HNM 标本用于配对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染实验。在指定时间点进行组织学、逆转录定量聚合酶链反应、酶联免疫吸附测定和批量 RNA 测序。结果培养的 HNM 在短期离体培养过程中保持了结构和功能的完整性。HDM 刺激增强了分泌活性,并在第 3 天诱导了 IL-4 和 IL-5 表达。两种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体(HU-1 和 Omicron BA.2)在表达血管紧张素转换酶 2 和跨膜蛋白酶丝氨酸 2 的 HNM 中产生了可检测到的病毒 RNA 和病毒 N 蛋白信号。病毒接种诱导了干扰素相关的 抗病毒药物 反应,该反应在以下情况下最为明显HU-1 暴露,尽管测量的 抗病毒药物 标记的变体间差异并不显着。在标准化感染条件下感染后 3 天,HU-1 在第 3 天表现出比 BA.2 更高的病毒 RNA 负荷和更明显的上皮损伤。讨论离体 HNM 模型保留了天然鼻粘膜的关键结构和细胞特征,包括完整的上皮屏障、基质免疫细胞和腺体成分,支持其用作补充传统二维培养物的组织水平平台。HDM 致敏的 HNM 显示出与 Der p 1 相关的 2 型反应,而病毒臂则显示出 抗病毒药物 反应以及探索性变异相关的上皮损伤差异。结论 短期离体 HNM 可能为研究上呼吸道对过敏原暴露和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的反应提供实用的组织水平平台。它保留了多细胞组织结构,同时在更大、更好分层的 队列研究 和扩展培养优化中进一步验证将支持更广泛的机械和转化应用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在模拟鼻腔对吸入病原体的炎症反应,重点关注鼻粘膜作为此类反应主要部位的作用。
设计与对象
该研究使用了来自屋尘螨(HDM)过敏性鼻炎患者的六份人类鼻粘膜样本和八份慢性鼻窦炎样本进行严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染实验。
核心方法
在指定的时间点进行组织学、逆转录定量聚合酶链反应、酶联免疫吸附测定和批量 RNA 测序,以评估组织反应。
主要结果
培养的 HNM 在短期离体培养过程中保持了结构和功能的完整性。感染后 3 天,HU-1 比 BA.2 显示出更高的病毒 RNA 负荷和更明显的上皮损伤。
解释与创新
离体 HNM 模型保留了天然鼻粘膜的关键结构和细胞特征,支持其用作组织水平平台来研究上呼吸道对过敏原和 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的反应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种离体模型对于理解和制定对抗 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 和过敏原引起的上呼吸道炎症的策略可能很有价值。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于小样本量和短期培养,这可能会限制普遍性。然而,考虑到 HNM 中病毒 RNA 和 抗病毒药物 反应的直接观察,证据是强有力的。

Application of a Human Nasal Mucosa Ex Vivo Culture System to Model Inflammatory Responses

Authors: J. Lai, Hao-Ran Peng, Shi-Can Zhou, Chun-Yan Yao, Zhi-Li Zhang, Kai C. Fan, Ya-Wen Gao, Ling Jin, Zheng-Liang Gao, Ping Zhao
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The nasal cavity is the entry point of the upper respiratory tract and the primary site of inflammatory responses to inhaled pathogens. Methods Human nasal mucosa (HNM) tissues were obtained from patients undergoing endoscopic nasal surgery. Six HNM specimens from patients with house dust mite (HDM)-allergic rhinitis were used for paired Der p 1 challenge experiments, and 8 chronic rhinosinusitis–derived HNM specimens were used for paired SARS-CoV-2 infection experiments. Histology, Reverse Transcription Quantitative Polymerase Chain Reaction, Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay, and bulk RNA sequencing were performed at the indicated time points. Results Cultured HNM maintained structural and functional integrity during short-term ex vivo culture. HDM stimulation enhanced secretory activity and induced IL-4 and IL-5 expression at day 3. Both SARS-CoV-2 variants (HU-1 and Omicron BA.2) produced detectable viral RNA and viral N-protein signals in HNM expressing Angiotensin-Converting Enzyme 2 and Transmembrane Protease Serine 2. Viral inoculation induced an interferon-associated antiviral response, which was most pronounced following HU-1 exposure, although between-variant differences in the measured antiviral markers were not significant. At 3 days postinfection under standardized infection conditions, HU-1 showed higher viral RNA burden and more marked epithelial injury than BA.2 at day 3. Discussion The ex vivo HNM model preserved key structural and cellular features of native nasal mucosa, including an intact epithelial barrier, stromal immune cells, and glandular components, supporting its use as a tissue-level platform that complements conventional 2-dimensional cultures. HDM-sensitized HNM showed Der p 1-associated type 2 responses, whereas the viral arm revealed an antiviral response and exploratory variant-associated differences in epithelial injury. Conclusions Short-term ex vivo HNM may provide a practical tissue-level platform for investigating upper-airway responses to allergen exposure and SARS-CoV-2 infection. It preserves multicellular tissue architecture, while further validation in larger, better-stratified cohorts and extended culture optimization will support broader mechanistic and translational application.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to model inflammatory responses in the nasal cavity to inhaled pathogens, focusing on the role of the nasal mucosa as the primary site of such responses.
Design and population
The study used six human nasal mucosa specimens from patients with house dust mite (HDM)-allergic rhinitis and eight chronic rhinosinusitis-derived specimens for SARS-CoV-2 infection experiments.
Core methods
Histology, Reverse Transcription Quantitative Polymerase Chain Reaction, Enzyme-Linked Immunosorbent Assay, and bulk RNA sequencing were performed at the indicated time points to assess tissue responses.
Main results
Cultured HNM maintained structural and functional integrity during short-term ex vivo culture. At 3 days postinfection, HU-1 showed higher viral RNA burden and more marked epithelial injury than BA.2.
Interpretation and novelty
The ex vivo HNM model preserved key structural and cellular features of native nasal mucosa, supporting its use as a tissue-level platform for studying upper-airway responses to allergens and SARS-CoV-2.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this ex vivo model could be valuable for understanding and developing strategies to combat upper-airway inflammation caused by SARS-CoV-2 and allergens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on a small sample size and short-term culture, which may limit generalizability. However, the evidence is strong given the direct observation of viral RNA and antiviral responses in HNM.

了解 长新冠 患者的支持需求:新西兰奥特罗亚的定性研究。

Understanding the Support Needs of People With Long COVID: A Qualitative Study in Aotearoa New Zealand.
作者： Fiona McKenzie, Bailey Yee, Rochelle Ellison-Lupena, Ridvan Firestone, Lis Ellison Loschmann, Kim van Wissen, Mona Jeffreys
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
长新冠 是一种多系统状况,影响着越来越多的个体。对于新西兰 Aotearoa 的 长新冠 患者的支持需求知之甚少。
设计与对象
这项研究使用了一种基于斯坦福设计思维过程和知识动员方法的协同设计方法。参与者是通过专业网络和支持团体滚雪球般招募的。所有参与者都亲身体验过长新冠。
核心方法
具有 长新冠 生活经验的人受邀参加由研究人员主持的 2024 年 3 月至 9 月期间举办的为期 2 小时的研讨会。将具有 长新冠 生活经验的人作为共同研究者,确保了研究的设计和实施能够前沿他们的知识和经验。参与者被要求描述他们对 长新冠 的体验,并向小组描述他们的创作;对描述进行录音、转录并进行主题分析。
主要结果
Eight workshops were conducted, including 30 participants, between March and August 2024. Of these, 25 (83%) were female; of the 25 who provided their age, most were aged 45 to 64 years (n = 18, 72%), four (16%) were aged 25 to 44 years and three (12%) aged 65 years or older. Five key themes emerged: (i) lack of assistance; (ii) systemic barriers; (iii) absence of long COVID clinics; (iv) financial strain; and (v) low quality of care. A sixth theme identified personal coping strategies.
解释与创新
新西兰 Aotearoa 的 长新冠 患者面临着主要的护理障碍,包括服务不足、经济困难和医疗保健途径分散。研究结果支持改善 长新冠 的途径和初级卫生保健教育。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为 长新冠 患者开发更容易获得和协调的医疗模式提供信息,强调需要改进初级卫生保健中的支持系统和教育。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小,依赖于自我报告的经历,这可能会引入偏差。证据是温和的,因为它是基于有限数量的参与者的定性数据。

Understanding the Support Needs of People With Long COVID: A Qualitative Study in Aotearoa New Zealand.

Authors: Fiona McKenzie, Bailey Yee, Rochelle Ellison-Lupena, Ridvan Firestone, Lis Ellison Loschmann, Kim van Wissen, Mona Jeffreys
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Long COVID is a multi-system condition that affects an increasing number of individuals. Little is known about the support needs of people living with long COVID in Aotearoa New Zealand. This study aimed to explore the lived experiences and healthcare access challenges of people with long COVID to better inform service design. METHODS: Co-design methodology was used, informed by the Stanford Design Thinking process and knowledge mobilisation approaches. Participants were recruited via snowballing, through professional networks and support groups. People with lived experience of long COVID were invited to participate in a 2-h workshop, facilitated by researchers, conducted between March and September 2024. Participants were asked to create a depiction of their experience of long COVID and describe their creation to the group; descriptions were audio-recorded, transcribed and thematically analysed. The inclusion of people with lived experience of long COVID as co-investigators ensured that the study was designed and conducted to forefront their knowledge and experience. RESULTS: Eight workshops were conducted, including 30 participants, between March and August 2024. Of these, 25 (83%) were female; of the 25 who provided their age, most were aged 45 to 64 years (n = 18, 72%), four (16%) were aged 25 to 44 years and three (12%) aged 65 years or older. Five key themes emerged: (i) lack of assistance; (ii) systemic barriers; (iii) absence of long COVID clinics; (iv) financial strain; and (v) low quality of care. A sixth theme identified personal coping strategies. CONCLUSIONS: Individuals with long COVID in Aotearoa New Zealand face major barriers to care, including inadequate services, financial hardship, and fragmented healthcare pathways. Findings support improved pathways and primary health care education in long COVID. A person-centred, coordinated, and accessible model of care is needed. LIVED EXPERIENCE OR PUBLIC CONTRIBUTION: The study was designed with two people with lived experience of long COVID included as co-investigators, and a further one on the Advisory Board. All participants had lived experience of long COVID.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Long COVID is a multi-system condition that affects an increasing number of individuals. Little is known about the support needs of people living with long COVID in Aotearoa New Zealand.
Design and population
This study used a co-design methodology informed by the Stanford Design Thinking process and knowledge mobilisation approaches. Participants were recruited via snowballing, through professional networks and support groups. All participants had lived experience of long COVID.
Core methods
People with lived experience of long COVID were invited to participate in a 2-h workshop, facilitated by researchers, conducted between March and September 2024. The inclusion of people with lived experience of long COVID as co-investigators ensured that the study was designed and conducted to forefront their knowledge and experience. Participants were asked to create a depiction of their experience of long COVID and describe their creation to the group; descriptions were audio-recorded, transcribed and thematically analysed.
Main results
Eight workshops were conducted, including 30 participants, between March and August 2024. Of these, 25 (83%) were female; of the 25 who provided their age, most were aged 45 to 64 years (n = 18, 72%), four (16%) were aged 25 to 44 years and three (12%) aged 65 years or older. Five key themes emerged: (i) lack of assistance; (ii) systemic barriers; (iii) absence of long COVID clinics; (iv) financial strain; and (v) low quality of care. A sixth theme identified personal coping strategies.
Interpretation and novelty
Individuals with long COVID in Aotearoa New Zealand face major barriers to care, including inadequate services, financial hardship, and fragmented healthcare pathways. Findings support improved pathways and primary health care education in long COVID.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accessible and coordinated healthcare models for individuals with long COVID, highlighting the need for improved support systems and education within primary health care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted with a small sample size and relied on self-reported experiences, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it is based on qualitative data from a limited number of participants.

Persistent Fungal Mortality in the United States Following the COVID-19 Pandemic Peak, 2022-2025.

Persistent Fungal Mortality in the United States Following the COVID-19 Pandemic Peak, 2022-2025.
作者： Meagan K. Andrews, Johnathan A. Edwards, Farida B. Ahmad, Jodi A. Cisewski, Lauren M. Rossen, Mitsuru Toda, Jeremy A.W. Gold
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Fungal infection–associated deaths increased in the United States during the COVID-19 pandemic, but mortality patterns after the pandemic peak are not well-characterized.
设计与对象
我们分析了 2022-2025 年国家生命统计系统的多种死因数据。
核心方法
We defined fungal deaths as deaths with a fungal pathogen listed as an underlying or contributing cause of death and summarized deaths by year, month, pathogen, COVID-19 association, underlying cause of death, demographic characteristics, and US Census division.
主要结果
During 2022-2025, a total of 30 225 fungal deaths occurred (annual average = 7556). The annual age-adjusted mortality rate per 100 000 population increased from 1.2 in 2018-2019 to 1.5 in 2020 and 1.9 in 2021, and remained between 1.8 and 1.9 during 2022-2025. COVID-19–associated fungal deaths declined after early 2022, but overall fungal mortality remained stable. Mortality rates varied by age, sex, race and ethnicity, and region. The most common underlying causes of death among fungal deaths were malignant neoplasms, HIV disease, and diseases of the heart; the most commonly specified pathogens were Candida and Aspergillus.
解释与创新
Fungal mortality remained elevated after the COVID-19 pandemic peak despite declines in COVID-19–associated fungal deaths.
科研与公卫意义
继续努力监测与真菌病相关的 死亡率 对于提高诊断能力和 死亡率 更好地表征并最终减少真菌病的影响非常重要。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性。

Persistent Fungal Mortality in the United States Following the COVID-19 Pandemic Peak, 2022-2025.

Authors: Meagan K. Andrews, Johnathan A. Edwards, Farida B. Ahmad, Jodi A. Cisewski, Lauren M. Rossen, Mitsuru Toda, Jeremy A.W. Gold
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Fungal infection–associated deaths increased in the United States during the COVID-19 pandemic, but mortality patterns after the pandemic peak are not well-characterized. We analyzed national mortality data to assess whether fungal mortality remained elevated during 2022-2025 and to describe demographic, geographic, pathogen-specific, and underlying cause-of-death patterns after the COVID-19 pandemic peak. Methods: We analyzed National Vital Statistics System multiple-cause-of-death data for 2022-2025. We defined fungal deaths as deaths with a fungal pathogen listed as an underlying or contributing cause of death and summarized deaths by year, month, pathogen, COVID-19 association, underlying cause of death, demographic characteristics, and US Census division. We used 2018-2019 (prepandemic) and 2021 (peak) as reference periods for selected comparisons. Results: During 2022-2025, a total of 30 225 fungal deaths occurred (annual average = 7556). The annual age-adjusted mortality rate per 100 000 population increased from 1.2 in 2018-2019 to 1.5 in 2020 and 1.9 in 2021, and remained between 1.8 and 1.9 during 2022-2025. COVID-19–associated fungal deaths declined after early 2022, but overall fungal mortality remained stable. During 2022-2025, the most common underlying causes of death among fungal deaths were malignant neoplasms, HIV disease, and diseases of the heart; the most commonly specified pathogens were Candida and Aspergillus . Mortality rates varied by age, sex, race and ethnicity, and region. Conclusions: Fungal mortality remained elevated after the COVID-19 pandemic peak despite declines in COVID-19–associated fungal deaths. Continued efforts to monitor fungal disease–related mortality are important to inform efforts to enhance diagnostic capacity and surveillance to better characterize and ultimately reduce the impact of fungal diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Fungal infection–associated deaths increased in the United States during the COVID-19 pandemic, but mortality patterns after the pandemic peak are not well-characterized.
Design and population
We analyzed National Vital Statistics System multiple-cause-of-death data for 2022-2025.
Core methods
We defined fungal deaths as deaths with a fungal pathogen listed as an underlying or contributing cause of death and summarized deaths by year, month, pathogen, COVID-19 association, underlying cause of death, demographic characteristics, and US Census division.
Main results
During 2022-2025, a total of 30 225 fungal deaths occurred (annual average = 7556). The annual age-adjusted mortality rate per 100 000 population increased from 1.2 in 2018-2019 to 1.5 in 2020 and 1.9 in 2021, and remained between 1.8 and 1.9 during 2022-2025. COVID-19–associated fungal deaths declined after early 2022, but overall fungal mortality remained stable. Mortality rates varied by age, sex, race and ethnicity, and region. The most common underlying causes of death among fungal deaths were malignant neoplasms, HIV disease, and diseases of the heart; the most commonly specified pathogens were Candida and Aspergillus.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
Continued efforts to monitor fungal disease–related mortality are important to inform efforts to enhance diagnostic capacity and surveillance to better characterize and ultimately reduce the impact of fungal diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

基因组监测 揭示了跨设置的年龄结构 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 传播。

Genomic surveillance reveals age-structured SARS-CoV-2 transmission across settings.
作者： Moreno GK, Brock-Fisher T, Krasilnikova LA, Schaffner SF, Burns M, Casiello CE, Messer KS, Petros B, Specht I, DeRuff KC
摘要中文翻译了解呼吸道病毒 传播 需要将病原体基因组数据与详细的流行病学背景相结合。我们分析了超过 85,000 个 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组,这些 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组 与 2021 年 11 月至 2023 年 1 月在马萨诸塞州收集的流行病学数据相关。该数据集在 666 个环境中收集,包括学校、学院和专业护理机构,该数据集可以直接比较环境内的 传播 与社区范围的风险。传播 升高主要集中在特定年龄的亚人群中——学校中的年龄较大的青少年、大学里的本科生和护理机构的居民——其他群体(尤其是工作人员)中几乎没有额外的风险。年轻人,尤其是大学里的年轻人,在传播中发挥了不成比例的作用,新的病毒谱系始终首先在这一群体中扩张。病毒传播遵循可重复的城市到农村梯度,疫苗接种 与向前 传播 的减少相关。我们进一步估计了及时检测新出现变异所需的测序阈值。总之,这些发现揭示了 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 传播 中一致的人口和空间结构,可以为病毒性呼吸道病原体 基因组监测 提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
了解呼吸道病毒 传播 需要将病原体基因组数据与详细的流行病学背景相结合。
设计与对象
我们分析了 2021 年 11 月至 2023 年 1 月在马萨诸塞州收集的超过 85,000 个与流行病学数据相关的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 基因组,涉及 666 个环境,包括学校、学院和专业护理机构。
核心方法
该数据集收集于 666 个环境,包括学校、学院和专业护理机构,可以直接比较环境内的 传播 与社区范围内的风险。我们进一步估计了及时检测新出现变异所需的测序阈值。
主要结果
传播 升高主要集中在特定年龄的亚人群中——学校中的年龄较大的青少年、大学里的本科生和护理机构的居民——其他群体(尤其是工作人员)中几乎没有额外的风险。年轻人,尤其是大学里的年轻人,在传播中发挥了不成比例的作用,新的病毒谱系始终首先在这一群体中扩张。
解释与创新
总之,这些发现揭示了 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 传播 中一致的人口和空间结构,可以为病毒性呼吸道病原体 基因组监测 提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
病毒传播遵循可重复的城市到农村梯度,疫苗接种 与向前 传播 的减少相关。这些发现可以为 监测 和公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
然而,研究设计的局限性和偏差可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。

Genomic surveillance reveals age-structured SARS-CoV-2 transmission across settings.

Authors: Moreno GK, Brock-Fisher T, Krasilnikova LA, Schaffner SF, Burns M, Casiello CE, Messer KS, Petros B, Specht I, DeRuff KC
Original abstract (English)
Understanding respiratory virus transmission requires integrating pathogen genomic data with detailed epidemiological context. We analyzed >85,000 SARS-CoV-2 genomes linked to epidemiological data collected in Massachusetts from November 2021 to January 2023. Collected in 666 settings, including schools, colleges, and skilled nursing facilities, this dataset enabled direct comparison of within-setting transmission to community-wide risk. Elevated transmission was concentrated in specific age-defined subpopulations-older adolescents in schools, undergraduates in colleges, and residents in nursing facilities-with little additional risk among other groups, notably including staff. Young adults, particularly in colleges, played a disproportionate role in spread, and new viral lineages consistently expanded first within this group. Viral spread followed reproducible urban-to-rural gradients, and vaccination was associated with reduced onward transmission. We further estimated sequencing thresholds required for timely detection of emerging variants. Together, these findings reveal consistent demographic and spatial structure in SARS-CoV-2 transmission that can inform viral respiratory pathogen genomic surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Understanding respiratory virus transmission requires integrating pathogen genomic data with detailed epidemiological context.
Design and population
We analyzed >85,000 SARS-CoV-2 genomes linked to epidemiological data collected in Massachusetts from November 2021 to January 2023, in 666 settings including schools, colleges, and skilled nursing facilities.
Core methods
Collected in 666 settings, including schools, colleges, and skilled nursing facilities, this dataset enabled direct comparison of within-setting transmission to community-wide risk. We further estimated sequencing thresholds required for timely detection of emerging variants.
Main results
Elevated transmission was concentrated in specific age-defined subpopulations-older adolescents in schools, undergraduates in colleges, and residents in nursing facilities-with little additional risk among other groups, notably including staff. Young adults, particularly in colleges, played a disproportionate role in spread, and new viral lineages consistently expanded first within this group.
Interpretation and novelty
Together, these findings reveal consistent demographic and spatial structure in SARS-CoV-2 transmission that can inform viral respiratory pathogen genomic surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
Viral spread followed reproducible urban-to-rural gradients, and vaccination was associated with reduced onward transmission. These findings can inform surveillance and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
However, the study design limitations and bias may affect the generalizability of the findings.

作者更正:严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 mRNA 疫苗 使肿瘤对免疫检查点阻断敏感。

Author Correction: SARS-CoV-2 mRNA vaccines sensitize tumours to immune checkpoint blockade.
作者： Adam Grippin, Christiano Marconi, Sage Copling, Nan Li, Chen Braun, Cole L Woody, Elliana Young, Priti Gupta, Min Wang, Annette Wu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决纠正原始出版物中有关 疫苗 给药时间和图中颜色编码的错误的需要,因为这些问题可能会影响结果的解释。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对原始文章中数据呈现错误的纠正,该文章发表在《自然》杂志上,重点关注 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 mRNA 疫苗 及其对癌症患者免疫检查点阻断的影响。
核心方法
更正包括更新文本和图形,以准确反映 疫苗 管理的时间和图中痕迹的颜色编码,以及修改致谢部分以包含资金信息。
主要结果
校正的主要结果是修改了 疫苗 给药时间(第 21 天而不是第 17 天)以及图中红色和蓝色迹线的校正颜色编码,其中红色迹线现在代表非 疫苗 组,蓝色迹线代表 疫苗 组。
解释与创新
作者认为这些更正是确保数据呈现的准确性和清晰度所必需的,新颖之处在于研究结果的更新报告。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于临床研究中 疫苗 相关数据的准确解释具有重要意义,这对于了解免疫检查点阻断在癌症 疫苗 中的功效至关重要。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于对原始出版物中错误的更正,并且对更正数据的置信度很高,因为所做的更改是为了确保准确性。

Author Correction: SARS-CoV-2 mRNA vaccines sensitize tumours to immune checkpoint blockade.

Authors: Adam Grippin, Christiano Marconi, Sage Copling, Nan Li, Chen Braun, Cole L Woody, Elliana Young, Priti Gupta, Min Wang, Annette Wu
Original abstract (English)
In the version of this article initially published, in Fig. 2d, the red triangle depicting vaccine administration at day 17 originally appeared at day 20; in Fig. 5o, the colours of the red and blue traces in the plot were interchanged; in Extended Data Fig. 3h,i, the red and blue colour keys (currently, blue for vaccine, red for no vaccine) were interchanged. 3b legend, in the text now reading “vaccine days 3, 6, 21,” 21 replaces the original “17,” and in the Fig. 3k legend, now reading “vaccine days 3, 6, 20,” 20 replaces the original “21.” In the first sentence of the Methods “Cox proportional hazards regression” section, in the text now reading “For Cox proportional hazards regression, exposures defined relative to an index date...,” “exposures defined relative to an index date” replaces the original “time-dependent variables were defined.” In the first paragraph of the Methods and in the Reporting Summary, data collection timelines have been updated to say “The data-collection cut-off was 1 September 2024 for the NSCLC and melanoma datasets and 1 January 2025 for the tissue agnostic dataset.” Finally, the Acknowledgements have been amended to include the statement that G.A. were supported by the CPRIT Research Training Award CPRIT Training Program (RP210028). The changes have been made in the HTML and PDF versions of the article.

Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for correcting errors in the original publication regarding the timing of vaccine administration and color coding in figures, as these issues could affect the interpretation of results.
Design and population
The study involved a correction of data presentation errors in the original article, which was published in Nature and focused on SARS-CoV-2 mRNA vaccines and their impact on immune checkpoint blockade in cancer patients.
Core methods
The correction included updating the text and figures to accurately reflect the timing of vaccine administration and the color coding of traces in plots, as well as amending the acknowledgments section to include funding information.
Main results
The main results of the correction were the revised timing of vaccine administration (day 21 instead of day 17) and the corrected color coding of the red and blue traces in the plot, with the red trace now representing the no-vaccine group and the blue trace representing the vaccine group.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the corrections as necessary to ensure the accuracy and clarity of the data presentation, and the novelty lies in the updated reporting of the study's findings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for the accurate interpretation of vaccine-related data in clinical studies, which is crucial for understanding the efficacy of immune checkpoint blockade in cancer treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to the correction of errors in the original publication, and the confidence in the corrected data is high as the changes were made to ensure accuracy.

Neurological manifestations in pediatric COVID-19: a comparative single-center study.

Neurological manifestations in pediatric COVID-19: a comparative single-center study.
作者： Hanan Ibrahim, Mervat Mansour, Ahmed Ragab Ahmed, Marwa Rushdy, Mona Mostafa ElGammal, Raghda Mohamed Hesham Zaitoun
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较有神经系统受累和无神经系统受累的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染住院儿童的临床特征、调查、治疗和结果,以确定神经系统疾病是否反映了全身严重程度或独特的临床模式。
设计与对象
一项回顾性观察研究在一家三级儿科中心进行,研究对象为实验室​​确诊的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的 1 个月至 16 岁儿童。患者被分为两组:有神经系统表现的组(ND 组,n = 24)和无神经系统受累的组(非 ND 组,n = 23)。
核心方法
比较两组之间的人口统计学特征、临床表现、实验室和神经影像学结果、治疗 和结果。该研究包括对医疗记录的回顾性分析,并利用神经影像学来识别特定的神经异常。
主要结果
患有神经系统受累的儿童年龄明显较大(中位数为 5.75 岁与 3.00 岁,p = 0.022),并且不太可能出现发烧或全身炎症特征。他们表现出较低的炎症标志物和显着较少的心脏受累,包括扩张型心肌病。神经影像显示腔隙性梗死、脱髓鞘性白质病变、纵向广泛的横贯性脊髓炎和马尾神经根增强。尽管 死亡率 发生率相似,但神经系统受累儿童的不完全恢复率显着较高,超过四分之一的儿童出现持续性神经系统后遗症(29.2% vs. 29.2%)。
解释与创新
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that neurological manifestations of pediatric COVID-19 represent a clinically distinct pattern characterized by limited systemic inflammation but an increased risk of persistent neurological sequelae.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,即使没有严重的全身性疾病,也需要对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的儿童进行早期神经学评估、适当的神经影像学和结构化的长期随访。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和单中心设置而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Neurological manifestations in pediatric COVID-19: a comparative single-center study.

Authors: Hanan Ibrahim, Mervat Mansour, Ahmed Ragab Ahmed, Marwa Rushdy, Mona Mostafa ElGammal, Raghda Mohamed Hesham Zaitoun
Original abstract (English)
Objective To compare clinical characteristics, investigations, management, and outcomes of hospitalized children with SARS-CoV-2 infection with and without neurological involvement, and to determine whether neurological disease reflects systemic severity or a distinct clinical pattern. Methods We conducted a retrospective observational study of children aged 1 month-16 years with laboratory-confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection admitted to a tertiary pediatric center between June 2022 and June 2023. Patients were divided into those with neurological manifestations (ND group, n = 24) and those without neurological involvement (non-ND group, n = 23). Demographic features, clinical presentation, laboratory and neuroimaging findings, treatment, and outcomes were compared. Results Children with neurological involvement were significantly older (median 5.75 vs. 3.00 years, p = 0.022) and less likely to present with fever or systemic inflammatory features. They demonstrated lower inflammatory markers and significantly less cardiac involvement, including dilated cardiomyopathy. Common neurological presentations included encephalopathy (50%), headache (45.8%), and seizures (20.8%). 37% met MIS-C criteria. Neuroimaging revealed lacunar infarctions, demyelinating white matter lesions, longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis, and cauda equina nerve root enhancement. Despite similar mortality rates, children with neurological involvement had a significantly higher rate of incomplete recovery, with more than a quarter experiencing persistent neurological sequelae (29.2% vs. Conclusion Neurological manifestations of pediatric COVID-19 represent a clinically distinct pattern characterized by limited systemic inflammation but an increased risk of persistent neurological sequelae. Early neurological evaluation, appropriate neuroimaging, and structured long-term follow-up are warranted even in the absence of severe systemic illness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare clinical characteristics, investigations, management, and outcomes of hospitalized children with SARS-CoV-2 infection who had neurological involvement versus those without, to determine whether neurological disease reflects systemic severity or a distinct clinical pattern.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted at a tertiary pediatric center, involving children aged 1 month to 16 years with laboratory-confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection. Patients were divided into two groups: those with neurological manifestations (ND group, n = 24) and those without neurological involvement (non-ND group, n = 23).
Core methods
Demographic features, clinical presentation, laboratory and neuroimaging findings, treatment, and outcomes were compared between the two groups. The study included a retrospective analysis of medical records and utilized neuroimaging to identify specific neurological abnormalities.
Main results
Children with neurological involvement were significantly older (median 5.75 vs. 3.00 years, p = 0.022) and less likely to present with fever or systemic inflammatory features. They demonstrated lower inflammatory markers and significantly less cardiac involvement, including dilated cardiomyopathy. Neuroimaging revealed lacunar infarctions, demyelinating white matter lesions, longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis, and cauda equina nerve root enhancement. Despite similar mortality rates, children with neurological involvement had a significantly higher rate of incomplete recovery, with more than a quarter experiencing persistent neurological sequelae (29.2% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that neurological manifestations of pediatric COVID-19 represent a clinically distinct pattern characterized by limited systemic inflammation but an increased risk of persistent neurological sequelae.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for early neurological evaluation, appropriate neuroimaging, and structured long-term follow-up for children with SARS-CoV-2 infection, even in the absence of severe systemic illness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and single-center setting, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature and potential for confounding factors.
